## 1. Refuge, Bodhicitta, Invocation, Blessing the Foundation, and so on. ^1-0

DAK SOK NAM KAY TA DANG NYAM PAY SEM CHEN TAM CHAY CHOK CHU DU SUM GYI DE SHIN SHEK PA TAM CHAY KYI KU SUNG TUK YÖN TEN TRIN LAY TAM CHAY KYI NGO WOR GYUR PA CHÖ KYI PUNG PO TONG TRAK GYE CHU TSA SHI’I JUNG NAY ^1-1

PAK PAY GEN DUN TAM CHAY KYI NGAB DAK DRIN CHEN TSA WA DANG GYÜ PAR CHAY PAY PAL DEN LA MA DAM PA NAM LA KYAB SU CHIO ^1-2

YI DAM KYIL KOR GYI LHA TSOK NAM LA KYAB SU CHIO ^1-3

SANG GYE CHOM DEN DAY NAM LA KYAB SU CHIO ^1-4

DAM PAY CHÖ NAM LA KYAB SU CHIO ^1-5

PAK PAY GEN DUN NAM LA KYAB SU CHIO ^1-6

PA WO KAN DRO CHÖ KYONG SUNG MAY TSOK YE SHE KYI CHEN DANG DEN PA NAM LA KYAB SU CHIO ^1-7

SANG GYE CHÖ DANG TSOK KYI CHOK NAM LA
JANG CHUP BAR DU DAK NI KYAB SU CHI
DAK GI JIN SOK GYI PAY SÖ NAM KYI
DRO LA PEN CHIR SANG GYE DRUP PAR SHOK ^1-8

SEM CHEN TAM CHAY DAY WA DANG DAY WAY GYU DANG DEN PAR GYUR CHIK ^1-9

DU NGAL DANG DU NGAL GYI GYU DANG DREL WAR GYUR CHIK ^1-10

DU NGAL MAY PAY DAY WA DAM PA DANG MIN DREL WAR GYUR CHIK ^1-11

NYE RING CHAK DANG NYI DANG DREL WAY TANG NYOM LA NAY PAR GYUR CHIK ^1-12

KUN CHOK SUM GYI DEN PA DANG ^1-13

SANG GYE DANG JANG CHUP SEM PA NAM KYI SHING DAG PAY JOR WA LA SOK PA SANG GYE KYI SHING YONG SU JANG PAY TU DANG ^1-14

DAK CHAK NAM KYI MÖ PAY TU LAY NAY DIR SANG GYE KYI SHING TAM CHAY KYI GYEN DANG KÖ PA DANG YÖN TEN PUN SUM TSOK PA TAM CHAY DANG DEN PAR GYUR CHIK ^1-15

TAM CHAY DU NI SA SHI DAK
SEK MA LA SOK MAY PA DANG
LAK TIL TAR NYAM BAI DUR YAY
RANG SHIN JAM POR NAY GYUR CHIK ^1-16

PAK SAM SHING GI KYE MÖ TSAL
SANG GYE DANG NI SANG GYE SAY
CHÖ NYEN DROK PAY GANG WA YI
CHOK NAM TAM CHAY GANG WAR SHOK ^1-17

KOR GYI KYIL KOR YÖ GUR YANG
JANG CHUP SEM PA MANG PO DAK
RANG GI LEK PAY YÖN TEN GYI
GYEN PAR DZAY CHING SHUK GYUR CHIK ^1-18

LÜ CHEN KÜN GYI JA DANG NI
SHING DANG Ö ZER TAM CHAY DANG
NAM KHA LAY KYANG CHÖ KYI DRA
GYUN MI CHAY PA DROK PAR SHOK ^1-19

HUNG! MA LÜ SEM CHEN KÜN GYI GÖN GYUR CHING
DÜD DE PUNG CHAY MI ZAY JOM DZAY LA
NGÖ NAM MA LÜ JI SHIN KHYEN GYUR PAY
CHOM DEN KOR CHAY NAY DIR SHEK SU SÖL ^1-20

CHOM DEN KAL PA DRANG MAY DU MA RU
DRO LA TSE CHIR TU JE NAM JANG SHING
MÖN LAM GYA CHEN GONG PA YONG DZOK PA
KYE SHE DRO DÖN DZAY DU DIR LAK NA ^1-21

DE CHIR CHÖ YING PO DRANG LHUN DRUP NAY
DZU TRUL JIN LAP NA TSOK TÖN DZAY CHING
TA YAY SEM CHEN TSOK NAM DRAL WAY CHIR
YONG DAK KOR DANG CHAY TE SHEK SU SÖL ^1-22

CHÖ NAM KUN GYI WANG CHUK TSO
TSO MA SER GYI DOK DRA SHING
NYI MA LAY LAK ZI JI CHE
DAY PAY CHEN NI DRANG WAR GYI ^1-23

SHI SHING TU JE CHE DEN PA
DUL SHING SAM TEN SA LA SHUK
CHÖ DANG YE SHE CHAK DRAL WA
KUN TU MI ZAY NAY PAR DEN ^1-24

TSUR JÖN TSUR JÖN SHING DAK LHA
TUP PA KYEM CHOK TAM CHAY KYEN
SHIN TU LEK JAY ZUK NYEN NI
CHÖ PAY NAY DIR SHEK SU SÖL ^1-25

DIR NI ZUK DANG LHEN CHIK TU
DRO WAY DÖN DU SHUK NAY KYANG
NAY MAY TSE DANG WANG CHUK DANG
CHOK NAM LEK PAR TSAL DU SÖL ^1-26

CHOM DEN TU JE JÖN PA LEK
DAK CHAK SÖ NAM KAL PAR DEN
DAK GI CHÖ YÖN SHE NAY KYANG
DI NYID DU NI SHUK SU SÖL ^1-27

TONG SUM KUN TU NYAM PA YI
PE MA DAP GYE GE SAR CHAY
DE SHING YANG PAR BÜL LAK NA
CHI DER BAR NI SHUK SU SÖL ^1-28

JI TAR TAM PA TSAM GYI NI
LHA NAM KYI NI TRÜ SÖL TAR
LHA YI CHU NI DAK PA YI
DE SHIN DAK GI KU TRÜ SÖL ^1-29

DI NI TRÜ CHOK PAL DANG DEN
TU JEY CHU NI LA NA MAY
JIN LAP YE SHE CHU YI NI
CHI DÖ NGÖ DRUP TSÖL WAR DZÖ ^1-30

DE SHIN SHEK PA NAM DANG DE SAY LA
RIN CHEN BUM PA MANG PO PÖ KYI CHÜ
YID ONG LEK PAR KANG WAY LU DANG NI
RÖL MAR CHAY PA DU MAY KU TRÜ SÖL ^1-31

GYAL WAY KU SUNG TUK LA NYÖN MONG MI NGA YANG
SEM CHEN LÜ NGAK YID SUM DRI MA DAK JAY CHIR
GYAL WAY KU SUNG TUK LA TRÜ CHAB DI SÖL WAY
SEM CHEN LÜ NGAK YID SUM DRI MA DAK GYUR CHIK ^1-32

DE DAK KU LA TSUNG PA MAY PAY GÖ
TSANG LA DRI RAB GÖ PAY KU CHI O
DE NAY DE LA KA DOK LEK GYUR WAY
NAB ZA SHIN TU DRI SHIM DAM LA BÜL ^1-33

GÖ ZANG SAB LA JAM PA NA TSOK DANG
GYEN CHOK GYA TRAK DE DANG DE DAK GI
PAK PA KUN TU ZANG DANG JAM YANG DANG
JIK TEN WANG CHUK SOK LA’ANG GYEN PAR GYI ^1-34

SAB JAM YANG PA LHA YI GÖ
MI CHE DOR JE’I KU DAK LA
MI CHE DAY PAY DAK BÜL NA
DAK KYANG DOR JE’I KU TOB SHOK ^1-35

NA TSOK WANG PO’I SHU TAR RAB TRA SHING
GANG LA REK NA DE WAY GYUR GYUR PA
GÖ ZANG RIN CHEN DAK LOB JONG CHIR BÜL
ZÖ PA DAM PAY GÖ KYI GYEN PAR SHOK ^1-36

DE LAY SHEN YANG CHÖD PAY TSOK
ROL MO YANG NYEN YI ONG DEN
SEM CHEN DU NGAL SEL JAY PAY
TRIN NAM SO SOR NAY GYUR CHIK ^1-37

## 2. The Seven Branches Prayer ^2-0

PAK PA JAM PAL SHÖN NUR GYUR PA LA CHAK TSAL LO ^2-1

JI NYE SU DAK CHOK CHU’I JIK TEN NA
DU SUM SHEK PA MI YI SENG GE KÜN
DAK GI MA LÜ DE DAK TAM CHAY LA
LÜ DANG NGAK YID DANG WAY CHAK GYI O ^2-2

ZANG PO CHÖ PAY MÖN LAM TOB DAK GI
GYAL WA TAM CHAY YID KYI NGÖN SUM DU
SHING GI DUL NYE LÜ RAB TU PA YI
GYAL WA KUN LA RAB TU CHAK TSAL LO ^2-3

DUL CHIK TENG NA DUL NYE SANG GYE NAM
SANG GYE SAY KYI U NA SHUK PA DAK
DE TAR CHÖ KYI YING NAM MA LÜ PA
TAM CHAY GYAL WA DAK GI GANG WAR MÖ ^2-4

DE DAK NGAK PA MI ZAY GYA TSO NAM
YANG KYI YAN LAK GYA TSOI DRA KUN GYI
GYAL WA KUN GYI YÖN TEN RAP DZÖ CHING
DE WAR SHEK PA TAM CHAY DAK GI TÖ ^2-5

ME TOK DAM PA TRENG WA DAM PA DANG
SIL NYEN NAM DANG JUK PA DUK CHOK DANG
MAR ME CHOK DANG DUK PÖ DAM PA YI
GYAL WA DE DAK LA NI CHÖ PAR GYI ^2-6

NAB ZA DAM PA NAM DANG DRIM CHOK DANG
CHE MA PUR MA RI RAB NYAM PA DANG
KÖ PA KYE PAR PAK PAY CHOK KUN GYI
GYAL WA DE DAK LA NI CHÖ PAR GYI ^2-7

CHÖ PA GANG NAM LA MAY GYA CHE WA
DE DAK GYAL WA TAM CHAY LA YANG MÖ
ZANG PO CHÖ LA DAY PAY TOB DAK GI
GYAL WA KUN LA CHAK TSAL CHÖ PAR GYI ^2-8

DÖ CHAK SHE DANG TI MUK WANG GI NI
LÜ DANG NGAK DANG DE SHIN YID KYI KYANG
DIK PA DAK GI GYI PA CHI CHI PA
DE DAK TAM CHAY DAK GI SO SOR SHAK ^2-9

CHOK CHU’I GYAL WA KUN DANG SANG GYE SAY
RANG GYAL NAM DANG LOB DANG MI LOB DANG
DRO WA KUN GYI SÖ NAM GANG LA YANG
DE DAK KUN GYI JE SU DAK YI RANG ^2-10

GANG NAM CHOK CHU’I JIK TEN DRÖN ME DAK
JANG CHUP RIM PAR SANG GYE MA CHAK NYE
GÖN PO DE DAK DAK GI TAM CHAY LA
KOR LO LA NA MAY PAR KOR WAR KUL ^2-11

NYA NGEN DA TÖN GANG SHE DE DAK LA
DRO WA KUN LA PEN SHING DAY WAY CHIR
KAL PA SHING GU DUL NYE SHUK PAR YANG
DAK GI TAL MO RAB JAR SÖL WAR GYI ^2-12

CHAK TSAL WA DANG CHÖD CHING SHAK PA DANG
JE SU YI RANG KUL SHING SÖL WA YI
GE WA CHUNG ZAY DAK GI CHI SAK PA
TAM CHAY DZOK PAY JANG CHUP CHEN POR NGO ^2-13

## 3. The Fifteen Dharma Practices of the Taklung ^3-0

CHÖ KYI GYAL PO KA NYAM DRO WAY GÖN
MI LOB ZUNG JUK GO PANG CHOK NYE PA
TSE CHEN GYU TRUL DRA WAY NAM ROL CHEN
PAL DEN TAK LUNG TANG PAR SÖL WA DEP ^3-1

LO BUR DRI MAY SHAL TEN NAM DROL DREN
NAM DAK KUM CHOK CHÖ KANG SANG SUM TÖN
JA Ö MIN GYUR TRI LA DREN PAY PAL
DAK NANG DE CHEN KU SUM CHAK TSAL LO ^3-2

DUL DREL GEN DUN U NA CHOM DEN DAY
YING DANG YE SHE NYI MAY LHEN CHIK KYE
TU JE CHEN PÖ DRO LA CHEN GYI ZIK
PA MO RANG NANG KU SHI CHAK TSAL LO ^3-3

TAB LA KAY SHING MIK MAY TU JE’I YING
DAY CHEN TRÖ DANG DREL WA RO CHIK KYANG
SI SHI DREN CHIR KA GYÜ RIM GYI JÖN
IN DRA BHU TI DANG CHAY CHAK TSAL LO ^3-4

DAY TONG NYI MAY ROL PAY PA WO CHIK
SHE RAB RANG SHIN DOR JE NAL JOR MA
DREN PA KU RU KUL LE DÖN KUN DRUP
CHÖ SUNG RIN CHEN ZANG DRUK CHAK TSAL LO ^3-5

CHIL DUN KA GYÜ GYU TSEN LHA DRUK LA
DI TO DRI MAY GE WAY DRO WA KUN
RIN CHEN NYE SHIN SEL MAY MI DREL SHING
NAM SUM YÖN TEN TRIN LAY CHIK GYUR SHOK ^3-6

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^3-7

DU SUM DE WAR SHEK PA CHÖ KYI KU
SÖL WA DEP SO TU JE’I CHEN GYI ZIK
SHEN WANG CHÖ LA BAR CHAY MAY PA DANG
DAM CHÖ RANG WANG CHÖ PAR JIN LAP SÖL ^3-8

YENG SUM TSE WA MAY PAY WEN NAY SU
JIK TEN TRI WA MA LÜ RAP PANG NAY
TSE CHIK DAM CHÖ NYING PO DRUP JAY PAR
NYUR DU JIN LOB TU JE’I CHEN GYI ZIK ^3-9

LÜ NGAK YID SUM LOK PAR DREN PA DANG
DRIP SUM MI GÜ SEM KYI BAR CHAY KUN
NAM SHI TRIN LAY TOK MAY NÜ TÜ YI
MA LÜ SHI SHING DAK PAR JIN LAP SÖL ^3-10

DREN SAM LAY DANG JA WA GANG JAY KUN
CHÖ DANG MI TÜN DRI MAY MI GÖ SHING
SHIN TU NAM DAK CHÖ SU LHUN DRUP PAR
RIN CHEN TU YI JIN GYI LAP DZAY SÖL ^3-11

RANG SEM DÖD NAY MA KYE NAM PAR DAK
LO BUR KYEN GYI MI JIK TOK GYUR NAY
Ö SAL CHÖ KU’I NGANG DU NYAM PA NYID
GYU DRAY NAM PAR DAK PA JIN LAP SÖL ^3-12

NAM DAK TRIN LAY BAY MAY LHUN GYI DRUP
GANG LA GANG DUL DE LA DER TÖN PAY
DUL JAY RE WA KONG WAR GYUN CHAY MAY
TOK PA MAY PAR JIN GYI LAP DZAY SÖL ^3-13

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^3-14

NYE PA TAM CHAY RAB ZAY CHING
YÖN TEN KUN GYI TER GYUR PA
DRAY BU TAM CHAY DZOK DZAY PAY
LA MA NAM LA CHAK TSAL LO ^3-15

CHÖ NYID NGANG DU SHEK GYUR CHING
CHÖ KU’I NGANG LAY MA YÖ KYANG
DAK CHAK LOB WANG DRO DRUK LA
NGÖN GYI DAM CHA MA YEL WAR ^3-16

TU JE’I CHEN PÖ TRIN DÜ TE
DAY LA SHEN PA MAY PAY NYAM
SAL LA DZIN PA MAY PAY DÖN
GYUN CHAY MAY PAY SAM TEN CHOK ^3-17

CHAR PA CHEN PO BAP TU SÖL
KYE MAY NGANG LAY MA YÖ KYANG
DAK CHAK JAY JUK SEM CHEN GYI
NAM TOK DAY DUK TRUL PA KUN ^3-18

NYAM NYID JAM PAY CHU WO YI
TSE WA TAM CHAY RAB SHI NAY
DAY WA CHEN POR NYAM DU SÖL
NANG MAY NGANG LAY NYAM NAY KYANG ^3-19

DAK CHAK SÖL WA DEP PA YI
GANG DANG GANG GI DÖ PA YI
DÖN NAM DRUP PAR DZAY DU SÖL
DAM PA KYE KYI TOK PA YI ^3-20

TSEN PAY NGÖ PO SHIK GYUR KYANG
DU SUM SAL WAR KYEN PA YI
DRUP PA JAY PAY GANG ZAK NAM
TUK KYI TSIR GYI ZUNG DU SÖL ^3-21

RAB SHI TRÖ PA NAM ZAY KYANG
DAK CHAK KA YI KOR WANG NAM
DREN PAR GYUR PA TSAM GYI KYANG
KYÖN DAK YÖN TEN DZOK DZAY SÖL ^3-22

DAY DUK NYAM PAR KYEN NA YANG
DU NGAL ZUK NGÜ NÖN PA YI
SEM CHEN DAY LA GÖ DZAY CHING
NYA NGEN SHI WAR DZAY DU SÖL ^3-23

DÜ SUM MA LÜ TAM CHAY DU
YENG WAY TSEN MA SHI NA YANG
JAY SU DRUP PAY GANG ZAK NAM
NGO WO NYID KYI RANG SEM LA ^3-24

YENG PA MAY PAR TAY NAY KYANG
NAK TRÖ WEN PA TAY WANG GI
TRAY CHING RANG WANG TEN DZAY SÖL
MA NAM BU LA SEM PA SHIN ^3-25

KYE KYI TU JE CHEN PO YI
DAK CHAK SEM CHEN MA LU PA
DU NGAL NAY LAY TAR DZAY NAY
TAM CHAY KYEN PA TOB DZAY SÖL ^3-26

JE RIN PO CHE YI JIN LAP KYI
DAK DANG TA YE SEM CHEN NAM
DE SHIN SHEK PAY YE SHE KYI
GANG DANG SHIN TU GANG WA DANG ^3-27

MIN DANG SHIN TU MIN DZAY SÖL
YI DAM CHÖ KYONG KÖN CHOK GI
DAK CHAK DRUP PA JAY PA LA
BAR CHAY TAM CHAY RAB SHI NAY ^3-28

TOK PA CHOK NYID TOB NAY KYANG
JIN LAP CHEN PO DZAY DU SÖL
JAM PAY CHU WO MI CHÖ CHING
NYING JE’I TSA WA RAB LAN TE ^3-29

JANG SEM JÖN SHING RAB GYE NAY
NYAM TOK ME TOK SHAR GYUR TE
DÖN NYI DRAY BU MIN DZAY SÖL
LA MA YI DAM KÖN CHOK DANG ^3-30

CHÖ KYONG KAN DRO KAR CHOK GYI
DAK CHAK YENG WAR GYUR PAY TSE
WEN PAR WANG MAY TRI PA DANG
CHI LA CHAK SHING SHEN PAY TSE ^3-31

MI LAM SHIN DU GO WA DANG
NANG LA SHEN CHING CHAK PA NAM
DÖD CHAK DAY WAR GYUR DZAY SÖL
DANG SEM JUNG WAR GYUR PA NAM ^3-32

NYING JER KYUR GYI GYUR WA DANG
MI SHE NYID MUK JING WA NAM
Ö SAL NANG MAY SHE RAB TU
TSO LA KA WA BAP PA SHIN
NGANG DU NAY PAR DZAY DU SÖL ^3-33

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^3-34

TA DRAL TRÖ MAY KA TAR NAM DAK KYANG
SI SHI WANG PO’I JE DRAK JI SHIN TU
TRIN LAY NA TSOK DU MAR TÖN DZAY PAY
TAB KAY TU JE CHE NGA CHÖ KYI JAY ^3-35

DU SUM DU MAY RIN CHEN YAB SAY LA
NAM SUM GÜ CHAK SHAB KYI PAY MOR TSAL
DAK SOK MONG LO’I MÜN PA RAB SAL NAY
TSA DREL SEM KYI DE NYID NGÖN GYUR SÖL ^3-36

MIK MAY NYING JE CHEN PO’I MA MA YI
DAK SOK DRO WA BU NAM MA LÜ PA
DRI MAY DAY CHEN YANG DAK NAY CHOK TU
NA TSOK TAB KYI DREN PAR DZAY DU SÖL ^3-37

NGÖN DÜ KA TSAL JI SHIN ZHU DZAY NAY
DU SUM DAY WAR SHEK PA CHÖ KYI JE
DAK SOK MÖ GÜ SAM PA TSIM PA DANG
TOK PA CHOK NYID NYUR DU KYE DZAY SÖL ^3-38

CHÖ JAY KA NI DRANG SONG CHEN PO’I SUNG
GYUR WA MI NGA DAY SHEK TU JE CHEN
GONG PA DZAY NAY DAK SOK MONG PA DI
DE SHIN NYUR DU JIN GYI LAP DZAY SÖL ^3-39

DAK SOK KA NYAM SEM CHEN TAM CHAY KYI
SAK PAY LAY DANG TRAL GYI KYEN NAM KUN
ME LONG YA DANG DRAL WA JI SHIN DU
MA LÜ DAK CHING DAK PAR DZAY DU SÖL ^3-40

TRUL DANG SAM NGEN MI TUN TRIN TSOK KUN
YE SHE LUNG GI TOR NAY DAK DZAY CHING
KA LA NYI KYIL GYE PA JI SHIN DU
KAR CHOK SAM PA NYUR DU DRUP DZAY SÖL ^3-41

TÖ CHING SÖL WA TAB PAY GAY WA DE
DAK SOK TSE DIR CHÖ KYI KU TOB NAY
TAK TU GEN DUN DÜ PA TUN PA DANG
GEN DUN YANG DAK DÖN CHEN DRUP PAR SHOK ^3-42

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^3-43

ZHI SOK PAY GÖL PUNG JOM PA WO TE
WANG CHUK LA SOK DRUK DEN KYEN TSER DEN
TSEN PE RAB GYEN YÖN TEN CHOK GI TRAY
YAB SAY TSUL SUM CHIK TÖN CHAK TSAL LO ^3-44

RAB TEN RI WO DANG DRA MIG YO WA
MI WANG DZAY PA TAR CHIN TSUNG PA MAY
GANG DÖN DZAY PA MI NGA NAM DROL DREN
DU SUM DAY SHEK YAB SAY KYÖ CHAK TSAL ^3-45

NAM SUM WANG CHUK SAM MAY SAM MI KYAB
YÖN TEN TRIN LAY TAR CHIN SAM DANG DREL
TEK SUM SI CHAY YO SHING CHOK LA GÖ
DREN PA CHÖ JE YAB SAY KYÖ CHAK TSAL ^3-46

DU SUM DAY SHEK NGO WO CHÖ JE’I TÜ
CHI NANG DRIP NÖ TSE WA RAB SHI NAY
PEN TSUN JAM PAY SEM DEN DAY CHEN TOB
SAM DRUP TEN PA DAR SHING GYE DZAY SÖL ^3-47

DI SOK DAK SHEN DU SUM TSOK SAK PAY
CHÖ YING SHAR CHOK DUL DREL DE WA CHEN
LO DRÖ DRI MAY GYAL PO TSE WA CHEN
TU JE RANG SHIN YAB SAY SHAB DRUNG DU ^3-48

DAK SOK LA MAY JANG CHUB CHOK TOB NAY
TSEN NYID CHIK GYUR BAY MAY TRIN LAY KYI
JI NYE DUL JAY TSOK NAM MA LÜ PA
LA MAY CHÖ JE’I GO PANG DREN PAR SHOK ^3-49

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^3-50

KYE PAR JANG CHOK GYU DIR CHEN RAY SIK
TSANG CHÖ TSUL GYI GYAL WAY TEN DZIN CHOK
PAL DEN TAK LUNG TANG PA CHEN PO YI
DRUP GYÜ NYIN JAY DZAM LING KYAB GYUR CHIK ^3-51

DÜ PA CHER PEL MI CHE KUN TU GAH
NAY DÖN BAR CHAY MING YANG MI DRAK LA
NAM PAR DAK PAY DU GO CHAR TAR BAB
TEN PA DZIN KYONG PEL WAR JIN GYI LOB ^3-52

KA GYÜ LA MAY JIN LAP DANG
YI DAM NGÖ DRUP DEN PA DANG
CHÖ KYONG SUNG MAY TU TSAL GYI
DRUP GYÜ TEN PA DAR GYE SHOK ^3-53

## 4. Indivisible Sixth Buddha Family ^4-0

RIK DRUK YER MAY DOR JE CHANG
KA BAB DRUK DEN TE LO PA
KA CHAY CHOK NYE NA RO PA
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-1

TRÜL PAY KYEM CHOK MAR LO’I TSEN
NAL JOR WANG CHUK MI LA RAY
GYAL WAY LUNG TEN GAM PO PA
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-2

KYEN PAY NGAB DAK PAK DRU PA
TEN PAY GYAL TSEN TAK LUNG PA
RANG JUNG SANG GYE RAT NAY TSEN
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-3

SHE RAB DRI MAY TU JE CHEN
GYÜ DE’I GONG PA KÜN DROL KIR TI SHRI
SHE JAY NAY LA MA MONG OR GYEN GÖN
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-4

DRUP GYÜ TEN PAY NGAB DAK GYAL WAY TSEN
MIK MAY NYING JE’I TER CHANG RAT NAY TSEN
NGE DÖN NYING PO’I DÖN TOK DRAK PAY TSEN
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-5

KYEN RAB WANG CHUK MIG YO GÖN
TRIN LAY LHUN DRUP GÖN PO’I TSEN
GYAL WAY NAM TRUL JIK TEN WANG
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-6

DA DREL TRUL KU PAD MAY TSEN
DOR JE DZIN WANG TSO KYE SHAB
KAY SHING DRUP NYE DRAK PAY SHAB
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-7

NGA WANG TRA SHI PAL DRUP SHAB
DRAK PA LEK DRUP CHÖ KYI JAY
KYEN RAB JAM PAL GYA TSO’I SHAB
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-8

TEN PA SAL DZAY KIR TI’I TSEN
RIK DAK TEN DZIN NAM GYAL SHAB
DRAK PA RIN CHEN SHE RAB PAL
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-9

TRA SHI DRAK PA GYAL TSEN SHAB
KYAB DAK RIN CHEN LEK DRUP DANG
KÜN DÜ CHÖ YING LHUN DRUP TSEN
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-10

TRA SHI CHÖ KYI GYAL TSEN SHAB
CHOK TRUL RIN CHEN Ö BAR TSEN
NGA WANG DRAK PA KUN SAL SHAB
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-11

TRIN LAY JAM PA JUNG NAY DANG
KUN GA NAM PAR GYAL WAY PAL
TSUNG MAY NGA WANG LEK DRUP SHAB
JE GONG MA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP ^4-12

DAM PAY TUK KYE NAM ROL NI

## 5. The Prayer called “Precious Body” ^5-0

JE RIN CHEN KU LA SÖL WA DEP
RIN CHEN KU YI JIN LAP KYI
DAK TA MAL NAM MIN LÜ GYA DI
KU JA LÜ DRI MAY TOB DZAY SÖL ^5-1

JE RIN CHEN SUNG LA SÖL WA DEP
RIN CHEN SUNG GI JIN LAP KYI
DAK CHAL PA NGAK GI DRI MA DAK
SUNG TSANG YANG DRUK CHU TOB DZAY SÖL ^5-2

JE RIN CHEN TUK LA SÖL WA DEP
RIN CHEN TUK KYI JIN LAP KYI
DAK SEM GYÜ BAK CHAK DRA WA DI
TUK YE SHE NGA PO TOB DZAY SÖL ^5-3

DAK SÖ NAM SAK PAY DRAY BU YI
LA MAY SHAB TOK CHOG GYUR TE
DE GÜ DEN PAY DRUP PA PÖ
TSOL DRAL GYI NYAM LEN TOB DZAY SÖL ^5-4

LA MA JIN LAP MI CHE GYU

KOR WAY SHI TSA CHÖ KEN YIN ^5-5

SANG SING CHAR TAR MI BAP GYU
SHEN PA NANG NAY LOK PA YIN

JAM SEM KYI TRIN LAY GYE PA YIN ^5-6

JE TSUNG MAY LA MA YAB SAY DANG
DAK DREL MAY CHI SUNG KA DRUP CHING
RANG SEM TRUL PA DAK PA DANG
DRO WAY DREN CHOK NÜ PAR SHOK ^5-7

NAM SHIK GYU LÜ JOK PAY TSE
BAR DO NANG WA MI CHAR SHING
NAY CHÖ DE WA CHAR WA DANG

DAR SHING GYE PAR GYUR CHIK TSUK LAK DAM PAY CHÖ

ZAY CHING TONG PAR GYUR CHIK KOR WA NGEN SONG SUM
SHI SHING DUL WAR GYUR CHIK LAY NGEN DÜD KYI DE ^5-9

JANG CHUP SEM PA GANG NA SU SHUK PA

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^5-12

DRO WAY GÖN PO MUN PA SEL DZAY PA
GYAL TSAB CHOK NYE WANG CHUK KYÖ LA DÜD ^5-16

KYE CHEN KYÖ KYI NAM TAR SHIN TU ZAP
LO LAY DAY PA JA JAY MI NGA YANG
DUL JAY TSOK LA JA JAY CHIR YANG NANG
NAM TAR SAM MI KYAB LA CHAK TSAL LO ^5-17

KYÖ KYI TSUL TRIM DRI MAY NAM PAR DAK
KYÖN DREL DRE PA MAY LA LHAK NAY KYANG
NGA’O ZAY PAY KOR SYM YONG SU DAK
TSUL TRIM NAM DAK KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^5-18

KA DANG NYAM PAY DRO WA MA LÜ KUN
MIN PAR DZAY PAY GO CHA RAB GÖ NAY
DRO NAM JANG CHUP CHOK LA GÖ PAR DZAY
TSUNG MAY TSE DEN KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^5-19

OR GYEN LA SOK NAY CHOK TAM CHAY KYI
PA WO NAL JOR TSOK NAM TAM CHAY DANG

JIK TEN TRÖ PA TAM CHAY RAB PANG NAY
DRANG SONG CHÖ PAY RI TRÖ WEN NAY TEN

TONG TÖ DÖN KUN DRUP LA CHAK TSAL LO ^5-21

KOR WAY GYAM TSO TONG PAR MA GYUR WAR
KAY CHIK TSAM YANG MIN DREL GYUR NAY KYANG

CHÖ KU TAK LUNG TANG PA LA
DRI GUNG JIK TEN GÖN PO YI

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^5-25

NAMO GURU RATNA NATHA YE ^5-26

NAM DAK DRI MA MAY PA RIN CHEN TUK

KYE LA CHAK TSAL SÖL TAB GE WA YI

JI TAR SAM DÖN YI SHIN DRUP PA DANG

RIN CHEN KYE LA GÜ PAY JIN LAB KYI

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^5-42

DE SHIN SHEK NAM YE SHE GYU MA SHIN
TRUL SHE DRAK PA DÖN DANG JE TUN PA

CHE DU JA WA MAY PAR RANG SHIN GYI

DO KAM NÖD CHUD TAM CHAY KAY CHIK LA

## 6. The Sutra Remembering the Three Jewels ^6-0

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^6-1

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^6-2

KÖN CHOK SUM LA CHAK TSAL LO ^6-3

RIK PA DANG SHAB SU DEN PA ^6-5

DE WAR SHEK PA ^6-6

JIK TEN KYEN PA ^6-7

KYE BU DUL WAY KA LO GYUR PA ^6-8

LA NA MAY PA ^6-9

LHA DANG MI NAM KYI TÖN PA SANG GYE CHOM DEN DAY TE ^6-10

DE SHIN SHEK PA DE NI SÖ NAM DAK GI GYU TÜN PA ^6-11

GE WAY TSA WA NAM CHÜD MI ZA WA ^6-12

ZÖ PA DAK GI RAB TU GYEN PA ^6-13

SÖ NAM KYI TER NAM KYI SHI ^6-14

PE JAY SANG PO NAM KYI TRAY PA ^6-15

TSEN NAM KYI ME TOK GYE PA ^6-16

CHÖDYUL REN WAR TÜN PA ^6-17

TONG NA MI TÜN PA MAY PA ^6-18

DAY PAY MÖ PA NAM LA NGÖN PAR GA WA ^6-19

SHE RAB ZIL GYI MI NÖN PA ^6-20

TOB NAM LA DZI WA MAY PA ^6-21

SEM CHEN TAM CHAY KYI TÖN PA ^6-22

JANG CHUB SEM PA NAM KYI YAB ^6-23

PAK PAY GANG ZAK NAM KYI GYAL PO ^6-24

NYA NGEN LAY DAY PAY DRONG KYER DU DRO WA NAM KYI DAY PÖN ^6-25

YE SHE PAK TU MAY PA ^6-26

POB PA SAM GYI MI KYAB PA ^6-27

SUNG NAM PAR DAK PA ^6-28

YANG NYEN PA ^6-29

KU JAY TA WAY CHOK MI SHE PA ^6-30

KU TSUNG PA MAY PA ^6-31

DÖ PA DAK GI MA GÖ PA ^6-32

ZUK DAG GI NYE WAR MA GÖ PA ^6-33

ZUK MAY PA DAK DANG MA DRE PA ^6-34

DU NGAL LAY NAM PAR DROL WA ^6-35

PUNG PO DAK LAY RAB TU NAM PAR DROL WA ^6-36

KAM NAM DANG MI DEN PA ^6-37

KYEM CHE NAM DAM PA ^6-38

DÜD PA NAM SHIN TU CHAY PA ^6-39

YONG SU DUNG WA DAK LAY NAM PAR DROL WA ^6-40

SI PA LAY DROL WA ^6-41

CHU WO LAY GAL WA ^6-42

YE SHE YONG SU DZOK PA ^6-43

DAY PA DANG
MA JÖN PA DANG ^6-44

DA TAR JUNG WAY SANG GYE CHOM DEN DAY NAM KYI YE SHE LA NAY PA ^6-45

NYA NGEN LAY DAY PA LA MI NAY PA ^6-46

YANG DAK PA NYID KYI TA LA NAY PA ^6-47

SEN CHEN TAM CHAY LA ZIK PAY SA LA SHUK PA TE ^6-48

DI DAK NI SANG GYE CHOM DEN DAY NAM KYI KU CHE WAY YÖN TEN YANG DAK PA NAM SO ^6-49

DAM PAY CHÖ NI TOK MAR GE WA BAR DU GE WA TA MAR GE WA ^6-50

DÖN ZANG PO
TSIK DRU ZANG PO ^6-51

MA DRE PA
YONG SU DZOK PA
YONG SU DAK PA
YONG SU JANG WA ^6-52

CHOM DEN DAY KYI CHÖ LEK PAR SUNG PA ^6-53

YANG DAK PAR TONG WA ^6-54

NAY MAY PA DÜ CHAY PA MAY PA NYE WAR TÖPA ^6-55

DI TONG WA LA DÖN YÖ PA ^6-56

KAY PA NAM KYI SO SOR RANG GI RIK PAR JA WA ^6-57

CHOM DEN DAY KYI SUNG PAY CHÖ DUL WA LA LEK PAR TÖN PA ^6-58

NGE PAR JUNG WA ^6-59

DZOK PAY JANG CHUP TU DRO WAR JAY PA ^6-60

MI TUN PA MAY CHING DÜ PA DANG DEN PA ^6-61

TEN PA YÖPAGYU WA CHAY PA’O ^6-62

TEK PA CHEN PO’I GEN DUN NI LEK PAR SHUK PA ^6-63

RIK PAR SHUK PA DRANG POR SHUK PA TÜN PAR SHUK PA ^6-64

TAL MO JAR WAY Ö SU GYUR PA ^6-65

CHAK JA WAY Ö SU GYUR PA ^6-66

SÖNAM KYI PAL GYI SHING ^6-67

YÖN YONG SU JONG WA CHEN PO ^6-68

JIN PAY NAY SU GYUR PA ^6-69

KUN TU YANG JIN PAY NAY SU GYUR PA CHEN PO’O ^6-70

SANG GYE YÖN TAN SAM MI KYAP
CHÖ KYI YÖN TEN SAM MI KYAP
PAK PAY GEN DUN SAM MI KYAP
SAM MI KYAP LA DAY JAY PAY ^6-71

NAM PAR MIN PA’ANG SAM MI KYAP
NAM DAK SHING DU KYE WAR SHOK ^6-72

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^6-73

## 7. The Third Event of the Day (Sojong Vow) ^7-0

TÖN PA CHOM DEN DAY DE SHIN SHEK PA DRA CHOM PA YANG DAK PAR DZOK PAY SANG GYE RIK PA DANG SHAB SU DEN PA ^7-1

DE WAR SHEK PA JIK TEN KYEN PA ^7-2

KYE BU DUL WAY KA LO GYUR WA ^7-3

LA NA MAY PA LHA DANG MI NAM KYI TÖN PA ^7-4

SANG GYE CHOM DEN DAY PAL GYAL WA SHA KYA TUP PA LA CHAK TSAL LO ^7-5

CHÖD DO KYAP SU CHI’O ^7-6

## 8. Praise and Prostration ^8-0

GANG TSE KANG NYI TSO WO KYÖ TAM TSE
SA CHEN DI LA GOM PA DÜN WAR NAY
NGA NI JIK TEN DI NA CHOK CHE SUNG
DE TSE KAY PA KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^8-1

NAM DAK KU NGA CHOK TU ZUK ZANG PO
YE SHE GYA TSO SER GYI LUN PO DRA
DRAK PA JIK TEN SUM NA LHAM ME WA
GÖN PO CHOK NYE KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^8-2

TSEN CHOK DEN PA DRI MAY DA WAY SHAL
SER DOK DRA WA KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO
DUL DRAL KYÖ DRA SI PA SUM MA CHI
NYAM MAY KYEN CHEN KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^8-3

GÖN PO TU JE CHE DEN PA
TAM CHAY KYEN PAY TÖN PA PO
SÖ NAM YÖN TEN GYA TSO’I SHING
DE SHIN SHEK LA CHAK TSAL LO ^8-4

DAK PAY DÖD CHAK DREL WAR GYUR
GE WAY NGEN SONG LAY DROL SHING
CHIK TU DÖN DAM CHOK GYUR PA
SHI GYUR CHÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^8-5

DROL NAY DROL WAY LAM YANG TÖN
LAB PA DAK LA RAB TU NAY
SHING GI DAM PA YÖN TEN DEN
GEN DUN LA YANG CHAK TSAL LO ^8-6

## 9. Sutra ^9-0

DIK PA CHI YANG MI JA SHING
GE WA PUN SUM TSOK PAR CHAY
RANG GI SEM NI YONG SU DÜL
DI NI SANG GYE TEN PA YIN ^9-1

KAR MA RAB RIB MAR ME DANG
GYU MA ZIL PA CHU BUR DANG
MI LAM LOK DANG TRIN TA BU
DÜ JAY CHÖNAM DE TAR TA ^9-2

## 10. Dedication ^10-0

SÖ NAM DI YI TAM CHAY ZIK PA NYID
TOB NAY NYE PAY DRA NAM PAM JAY SHING
KYE GA NA CHI’I BA LAP TRUK PA YI
SI PAY TSO LAY DRO WA DROL WAR SHOK ^10-1

## 11. The Praise of the Twelve Deeds of the Bhagavan, the Powerful Sage ^11-0

TAB KAY TU JE’I SHA KYA’I RIK SU TRUNG
SHEN GYI MI TUP DÜD KYI PUNG JOM PA
SER GYI LHUN PO TA BUR JI PAY KU
SHA KYA’I GYAL PO KYE LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-1

GANG GI DANG POR JANG CHUP TU KYE NAY
SÖ NAM YE SHE TSOK NYI DZOK DZAY CHING
DU DIR DZAY PA GYA CHEN DRO WA YI
GÖN GYUR KYE LA DAK GI TÖ PAR GYI ^11-2

LHA NAM DÖN DZAY DUL WAY DU KYEN NAY
LHA LAY BAP NAY LANG CHEN TAR SHEK NAY
RIK LA ZIK NAY LHA MO GYU TRUL GYI
LHUM SU SHUK PAR DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-3

DA WA CHU DZOK SHA KYA’I SAY PO NI
TRA SHI LUM BI’I TSAL DU TAM PAY TSE
TSANG DANG GYA JIN GYI TÖ TSEN CHOK GI
JANG CHUP RIK SU NGE DZAY CHAK TSAL LO ^11-4

SHON NU TOB DEN MI YI SENG GE KUN
ANGHA MA GA DHA RANA GYU TSAL DREN
KYE WO DREK PA CHEN NAM TSAR CHAY NAY
DREN DA MAY PAR DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-5

JIK TEN CHÖ DANG TUN PAR JA WAY CHIR
KA NA MA TO PANG CHIR TSUN MO YI
KOR DANG DEN DZAY TAB LA KAY PA YI
GYAL SI KYONG WAR DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-6

KOR WAY JA WA NYING PO MAY ZIK NAY
KYIM NAY JUNG TE KA LA SHEK NAY KYANG
CHÖR TEN NAM DAK DRUNG DU NYID LA NYID
RAP TU JUNG WAR DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-7

TSÖN PAY JANG CHUP DRUP PAR GONG NAY NI
NE RAN JA NI DRAM DU LO DRUK TU
KA WA CHAY DZAY TSÖN DRU TAR CHIN NAY
SAM TEN CHOK NYE DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-8

TOB MA MAY NAY BAY PO DÖN YÖ CHIR
MA GA DA YI JANG CHUP SHING DRUNG DU
KYIL TRUNG MIG YO NGÖN PAR SANG GYAY NAY
JANG CHUP DZOK PAR DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-9

TU JE’I DRO LA NYUR DU ZIK NAY NI
WA RA NA SI LA SOK NAY CHOK TU
CHÖ KYI KOR LO KOR NAY DUL JA NAM
TEK PA SUM LA GÖ DZAY CHAK TSAL LO ^11-10

SHEN GYI GÖL WA NGEN PA TSAR CHAY NAY
MU TEK TÖN PA DRUK DANG LHAY JIN SOK
KOR LO JIK PAY YÜL DU DÜD NAM TÜL
TUP PA YÜL LAY GYAL LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-11

SI PA SUM NA PE MAY YÖN TEN GYI
NYEN DU YÖD PAR CHO TRUL CHEN PO TEN
LHA MI DRO WA KUN KYI RAP CHÖ PA
TEN PA GYE PAR DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-12

LE LO CHEN NAM NYUR DU KÜL JAY CHIR
TSAM CHOK DRUNG GI SA SHI TSANG MA RU
CHI MAY DOR JE TA BU’I KUR SHEK NAY
NYA NGEN DA WAR DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-13

YANG DAK NYID DU JIK PA MAY CHIR DANG
MA ONG SEM CHEN TÖD NAM SAK JAY CHIR
DE NYID DU NI RING SEL MANG TRUL NAY
KU DUNG CHAB GYE DZAY LA CHAK TSAL LO ^11-14

DI TAR TEN PAY DAK PO CHOM DEN DAY
DZAY PAY TSUL LA DO TSAM TÖ PA YI
GE WAY DRO WA KUN GYI CHÖ PA YANG
DE SHEK NYID KYI DZAY DANG TSUNG PAR SHOK ^11-15

DE SHIN SHEK PA KYE KU CHI DRA DANG
KOR DANG KU TSE’I TSE DANG SHING DAM DANG
KYE KYI TSEN CHOK ZANG PO CHI DRA WA
DRI DRA KO NAR DAK SOK GYUR WAR SHOK ^11-16

KYE LA TÖD CHING SOL WA TAB PAY TÜ
DAK SOK GANG DU NAY PAY SA CHOK SU
NAY DANG ÜL PONG TAB TSÖ SHI WA DANG
CHO DANG TRA SHI PEL WAR DZAY DU SÖL ^11-17

TÖN PA JIK TEN KAM SU JÖN PA DANG
TEN PA NYI Ö SHIN DU SAL WA DANG
TEN DZIN PÖN LOB SHAY DRUP GYE GYUR NAY
TEN PA YUN RING NAY PAY TRA SHI SHOK ^11-18

## 12. Praise of the Sage ^12-0

NA MO SHA KYA MU NI YE ^12-1

GANG LA TEN NA SI SHI’I PAL
DE LAK DRUP PA SHA KYA’I TOK
SA SUM DRO WAY DREN PA DER
RAP TU GÜ PAY CHAK TSAL LO ^12-2

GAN DEN DZIN MAY LEK BU LA
SÖ NAM PIR GYI DU JAY DE
SHAB TIL RI MO LEK KÖD NAY
CHÖ KAR NANG WAY PEN DER DZAY ^12-3

TSAY MAY TU JE’I SHEN WANG GI
KYE KU NYI TUNG WANG PO’I TSUL
NYOK DREL LHUM GYI CHU TER CHER
DA CHU NYE WAR TEN JAY NAY ^12-4

LAY MAR YUM GYI TA ZUR NI
LHÜN PO’I PO LAY RAP TÖN PAY
NANG NYEN PAK SAM JÖN PA DER
TSEN PE’I YAL GA’I NAM PAR CHUK ^12-5

RIK PAY NAY DANG GYU TSAL GYI
NYIN JAY CHE CHER SAL WA NA
DREN SEM DUK PAY RAP LAY PAY
JUNG PO’I JA NAM DONG PAR JAY ^12-6

GA MA DRUK TRI’I NAM KUN TU
DÖ PAY YÖN TEN NGAY CHÖ KYANG
DAM DZAP TRÖ NA CHU KYE TAR
SI PAY SHAR YANG GÖ MA GYUR ^12-7

GYAL SAY LUNG ZANG LAY GAL TE
NAM DAK CHÖR TEN DRUNG SHEK NAY
KYÖ KU SER GYI LHUN PO NI
LA GÖ TSAM TRIN SHÖN NÜ KYÜ ^12-8

TSANG PAY DRANG SONG MA LÜ PA
CHIK TU JAY PAY PER MIN PAY
NAK KAY KA TUP LA NAY TÜ
NYER SHI’I DE WA NYE PAR GYUR ^12-9

NAM DAK DOR JE DEN PO DER
JANG NGÖN TSA YI TING WA LA
YO MAY KYIL TRUNG RAB CHAY PAY
TU YI DÜD YID LONG POR JAY ^12-10

MI DÖ JE WAY TOR LUNG GI
YI GA’I NAM TRÜL DE NAM KYI
MI TSAM DU MA DU GYI KYANG
JAM PAY TING NGE DZIN GYI CHOM ^12-11

PAK KA KOR WAY TSO CHEN LA
SAM TEN DRU DZING RAB CHAY TE
KAY CHIK NYID LA GAL WAY MÖ
JIK TEN SUM GYI LA MA GYUR ^12-12

SER NGAL CHEN GYI KOR LO CHAY
GÜ PAY SÖL WA TAB PAY NGOR
GE LONG NGAR CHAY LHA’I KOR Ü
DEN SHI’I CHÖ KYI GYAL NGA DUNG ^12-13

WANG CHU DEN PAY SEM PA DANG
MRA WAY KYUM CHOK NYEN TÖ TSOK
MÖ GE’I SHING SA SHIN PO DER
NGO WO NYID MAY CHÖ CHAR PAB ^12-14

TEK SUM KANG ZANG NYAM GA WAR
SHUK NAM JES SU ZUNG WAY LAY
TA MA LEK PAR NAM CHE WAY,
CHÖ KYI KOR LO RAP DU KOR ^12-15

DUL JAY PAY TSAL MA LÜ PA
TU JE’I NYI ÖD KYI KYANG TAR
SA LAY JÖN PA ZUNG GI Ü
NYA NGEN DAY PAY DZAY PA TEN ^12-16

RIN CHEN WANG GI GYAL PÖ KYANG
PER JAR MAY PAY RING SEL NAM
LHA DANG CHAY PAY JIK TEN GYI
KYE GU’I SÖD NAM DRÖN DU GYUR ^12-17

LAY MA’ANG SI PA JI SI BAR
NAM TRÜL DU MAY DÖ GAR GYI
DÜL JA DRANG YE MIN DZAY PA
TAK PAY TRIN LAY CHEN DER DÜD ^12-18

DE TAR SÖL WA TAB PAY TÜ
KYE KYI TEN PAY JUK NGOK SU
DAK CHAK DÜL JAR GYUR PA NAM
KÜN KYEN GO PANG TOB PAR SHOK ^12-19

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^12-20

## 13. Praise of the King of Sages and Disciples Called The Loud Sound of Drums That Awakens All Beings Through Great Equanimity ^13-0

KÖN CHOK GYA TSO’I JIN DANG TSOK NYI KYI
SAK PA NGÖN PAR DAR LA CHÖ KYI YING
NAM DAK SAM GYI MI KYAB DEN PA CHE
CHOK CHU’I SHING GI GYEN KÖ TA DAK PAY ^13-1

DZE PA CHIK TU NAY DIR TRUN PA LA
DA MAY TEN PAY DAK PO TUP PA CHE
PAK PAY NAY TEN TSOK KYI SHAB DRING CHAY
TUK DAM WANG GI SHEK TE SHUK SU SÖL ^13-2

TUK KYE MÖN LAM DZAY TOB ZAB GYA CHE
GYAL WA KUN GYI TOG TU TÖN PAY PAL
TEK SUM CHÖ KYI KOR LO GYUR WANG PO
DZOK SANG GYE LA NGÖ PO KÜN GYI DÜ ^13-3

DE SER PAL DRIN SÖ NAM KAR WAY ZUK
PEN DE’I YEN LAK GYA TSO JUNG WAY NAY
DUNG SEL NGA YAB NGE TUL PÖ POR DZIN
DRA CHOM TONG DANG SUM GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-4

DRUP PAY TEN PA DANG SHEL TSER WAY PUK
DIK TO DÜD KYI YÜL LAY MA PAM PA
NANG DU YANG DAK JOK GI JAY CHING CHEN
DRA CHOM GYA TRAK LOB MAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-5

LO MA DÜN DEN JÖN PAY NAK LUNG DU
ZAK PA KÜN ZAY PAK CHOK NAK NA NAY
SHI WAY DIK DZUP NOR BU’I NGA YAB CHEN
DRA CHOM TONG DANG SHIB GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-6

RIN CHEN YID TAR JUNG WA ZANG LING DU
DÜ SHIN DÜL JA DÜL WAY TSE DEN NYEN
SER GYI NA GYEN YID DU CHI WA DZIN
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-7

TROK JAY NYAM RANG GYU WA SENG GE’I SHIR
NAY TEN CHEN PO DOR JE MO YI BU
DIK DZUP SIL BU TER WAY NGA YAB DZIN
DRA CHOM TONG GI NYE KOR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-8

DAL BAB CHU LUNG YA MU NA YI NGOR
SI LAY NAM DROL NYE PA ZANG PO NI
TONG WAY DIK ZOL CHÖ CHAY NYAM SHAK CHEN
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-9

GE SAR ZANG PO’I KYE TSUL KASMI RAR
DENG CHEN RI KYI CHÖD NAY SER BE’U
TU JE’I TSEN TSÖN RIN CHEN SHAK PA TOK
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-10

ZUK ZANG RING TSO BA LANG CHÖ LING DU
DREN PA SHE RAB PUL JUNG SER CHEN CHOK
TING NGE DZIN LA NYOM PAR JUK PAY GYA
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-11

MÖN PA TRAL LA DRUP PA JANG LING GI
Ü MAR CHÖ MRA’I WANG PO BA KU LA
NA TSOK RIN CHEN JIN ZÖ NE’I LE DZIN
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-12

JOR GYE DE WA TRI YANG KU’I LING DU
GYAL SAY LAB LA RAB GÜ DRA CHEN ZIN
DRANG YAY RIN CHEN LAY GYI TROK SHU NAM
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-13

GYAL KAB JA GÖ PUNG PO DRAK PAY RIR
NYÖN MONG TSAY NAY RAB SHI LAM TREN TEN
SAM TEN CHE LA NYAM SHAK CHAK GYA CHING
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-14

NGA GYAR TSO WA LÜ KYAD PAK LING DU
DIK DREL BHA RA DHWA DZA SÖ NYOM LEN
KA YI LEK PAM LOK CHING LUNG ZAY NAM
DRA CHOM TONG GI PU DU CHAY LA DÜ ^13-15

SUM TSEN CHÖ JIN GYA PA’I TSUK GYEN DU
KUR TIR JAY PA ZUNG TOB LAM TEN NI
TEK CHOK DE NÖ PU TI CHEN GYI TSA
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-16

RAP JI DÜL TSEK NGÖ YANG CHE LENG WAR
SI SHI’I YANG SA LAY SUNG LU YI DE
TER GYI BUM PA ZANG DANG SIL JAY CHANG
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-17

BI HU LAR JÖ LHUN PO’I DZE GYEN DU
NYEN TÖ PAK PA TSAM DU BE JAY NI
GYAL WAY SUNG GI PU STI KA LA TOK
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-18

SIN DHU’I DRAM NGOK GANG CHEN RI YI POR
RIK DANG DRÖL WAY NOR CHEN MI CHE PA
CHÖ KU JANG CHUP CHEN PO’I CHÖ DONG DZIN
DRA CHOM TONG DANG NYI GYAR CHAY LA DÜ ^13-19

SI PAY MÜN CHEN KA NAY SHI WAR TAR
NYÖN MONG CHU DZIN NYEN PO’I LUNG GI TOR
ZAY DANG MI KYE SHE PA’I Ö TONG CHEN
PEN DE’I NYIN JE KOR GYI TSOK LA DÜ ^13-20

MA LÜ TUP PA’I TUK KYI SAY TU WO
CHEN RE ZI WANG NANG TA’I GYAY DAB CHEN
NGA YAP CHI LUK DZIN PA GE NYEN TSÜL
PAK PA NAM KYI SHAB DRING CHE DER DÜ ^13-21

DAY PAY WANG PO CHÖ PAY TRO WA CHEN
GYAL WA LO MAR CHAY PAY DÜN DAR NAY
CHE DU SHAM SHIN GYUN CHAY SI MIN PAY
JE PO HA SHANG CHEN PO DE LA DÜ ^13-22

SHAR DU CHÖ DEN YÜL KOR TOB KYI SUNG
LOR NI TEK CHEN PAK PA’I RIK KYE PO
NUP TU TEN DRA LA TRÖ MIK MI ZANG
JANG DU BÜL SAL DÖ GU’I NGAL SÖ PO ^13-23

DE TAR DAK PAY KYIL KOR CHIK NYI DER
TEN PA KYEN TOK TU JE’I TER DE DAK
DREN SAM GYI KYANG TEN DÜN MI CHUK PAR
SHE NAY KÜN TU DANG WAY CHAK GYI’O ^13-24

NGÖ SU JOR DANG YID KYI TRÜL PAY CHÖ
TO RI TAR PA’I GEK JAY DIK NAM SHAK
LAP CHEN TRIN LAY GYE PA’I TRO WA TAY
NEM NUR MAY PAR DAM CHÖ SUNG WAR SÖL ^13-25

YÜN DU TSO SHING SHE LA DRO DÖN DZAY
GE WAY LUNG DANG TOK PA’I TEN PA NI
DÜL JAY GYU LA DAR GYE TAK PA YI
CHE NGÖ DE SHIN DRUP PAR JIN GYI LOB ^13-26

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^13-27

## 14. The Tea Offering: Deathless Amrita ^14-0

KYAP KÜN DÜ TSUNG MAY SI SHI’I GÖN
CHOK TÜN MONG GI TER DZÖ RIN PO CHE
YAB DRIN CHEN TSA WAY LA MA LA
JA CHI MAY DÜD TSI’I CHÖ PA BÜL ^14-1

PAL KYEN TSE’I WANG CHUK DRO WAY GÖN
JE TAK LUNG TANG PA CHÖ KYI JE
KYE GYÜD PA RIN CHEN DANG CHAY LA
JA CHI MAY DÜD TSI’I CHÖ PA BÜL ^14-2

LHA RAP JAM GYÜ DE’I KYIL KOR DU
SHING GANG DÜL TUP WANG DZOK SANG GYE
SAY LOB MAR CHAY PA TAM CHAY LA
JA CHI MAY DÜD TSI’I CHÖ PA BÜL ^14-3

NGÖN TEN PA SUNG WAR NGA SÖL WAY
PAL YE SHE GÖN PO CHAM DREL DANG
LHA GE NYEN JI GYAL KOR CHAY LA
JA CHI MAY DÜD TSI’I CHÖ PA BÜL ^14-4

DAK DI NAY TSE RAP TAM CHAY DU
GÖN TSUNG MAY DAM PA KYE NAM DANG
DÜ KAY CHIK TSAM YANG MIN DREL SHING
CHÖ CHAK GYA CHEN PO CHOK TOB SHOK ^14-5

JE DREN CHOK CHÖ KYI GYAL PO NI
KU MIN GYUR DOR JE SENG GE’I TRIR
SUNG TSANG YANG CHÖ KYI CHAR BEP SHING
TUK TSE WAY DÜL JA KYONG DZAY SÖL ^14-6

MA SEM CHEN DE LA GÖ PA DANG
GYAL TUP PA’I TEN PA DAR WA DANG
KYEN MI TÜN PAR CHAY KÜN SHI NAY
CHOK SANG GYE KYI GYAL SI NYUR TOB SHOK ^14-7

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^14-8

## 15. Prayer to the Guru and Lineage ^15-0

GANG GI DRIN GYI DE CHEN NYID
KAY CHIK NYID LA CHAR WA GANG
LA MA RIN CHEN TA BU’I KU
DOR JE CHEN SHAP PAY LA DÜ ^15-1

MIN GYUR DE CHEN GYU MAY KU
SHE RAB MA DANG NYAM JOR SHING
SI DANG SHI WA KÜN GYI PAL
DOR JE CHANG WANG LA CHAK TSAL ^15-2

GANG GI TIL LAY MAR SHIN DU
LÜ NAY YE SHE CHEN PO NI
LEK TONG KAN DRO’I WANG CHUK TSO
JE TSUN TI LO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-3

KA CHAY KÜN GYI MI KYO WAR
KYEN DEN KYÖ NI KAY CHIK LA
LA MAY LAM GYI NAM PAR DRÖL
PAL DEN NA RO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-4

PAK YÜL GYA TSOR RAB SHUK SHING
DRUP CHEN LU WANG GÜ TEN NAY
NYEN GYÜ NOR BU’I DZÖD NGA WA
MRA GYUR MAR PA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-5

DRUP LA TSÖN PAY SHUK DRAK PÖ
NGÖN PAR TOK PA CHOK NYE PAY
DRUP PAY WANG CHUK KUN GYI JE
SHE PA DOR JE LA CHAK TSAL ^15-6

SHA KYA SENG GE LUNG TEN CHING
TSÜL NAY TÖ DANG SAM DEN PAY
DRUP GYÜ TEN PA’I GYAL TSEN DZIN
DA Ö SHÖN NU LA CHAK TSAL ^15-7

LUNG DANG RIG PA TUK CHÜD NAY
TOK PA CHOK GI RAB DRÖL TE
TÖN PA’I DAM CHÖ KÜN DZIN PA
DOR JE GYAL PO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-8

TSUL TRIM PUNG PÖ RAP GYEN CHING
TING NGE DZIN LA NGA NYE PAY
DREN PA’I DA DANG DREL GYUR PA
TAK LUNG TANG PA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-9

KA CHAK CHU WO NYI DRE KYI
SHING TA’I SÖL CHEN RAP DZIN PAY
DRO WA KÜN GYI GÖN GYUR PA
RATNA NATHA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-10

NYE PA KÜN LAY NAM DRÖL SHING
TSÜL TRIM GYEN GYI RAP DZE PA
MI NUP DRUP PAY GYAL TSEN DZIN
SHE RAB LA MA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-11

KYEN PA’I YE SHE RAP GYAY SHING
MIK MAY TSE WAY TER GYUR PA
TRIN LAY DZAY GYÜN MI NUP PAY
TRA SHI LA MA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-12

SHE JA KÜN LA RAP KAY SHING
NAM PAR DRÖL WA CHOK NYE PA
LUNG TOK TEN PA SAL DZAY PAY
SANG GYE PAL ZANG LA CHAK TSAL ^15-13

DRIP NYI BAK CHAK CHAY JANG SHING
TOK PAY YÖN TEN KÜN GYI TER
TU DANG TRIN LAY TA YE PA
RAT NA GU RU LA CHAK TSAL ^15-14

GÖN KYÖ TUK KYI BUM ZANG NI
KYEN TSE’I CHAB KYI TAM GYUR PAY
DRO KÜN MIN CHING DRÖL DZAY PA
RATNA KARA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-15

DO DANG GYÜ KYI SHUNG LUK LA
TÖ DANG SAM PA KÜN DZOK NAY
NYID TUK DRUP LA NAM SHÖL WA
KÜN PANG CHEN PO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-16

DÜ SUM SANG GYE KÜN GYI NGÖ
GYE TRI SHI TONG CHÖ KYI TER
PAK CHOK GEN DÜN GYA TSO’I TSO
TRA SHI PAL TSEK LA CHAK TSAL ^15-17

MA LÜ SANG GYE TAM CHAY KYI
KYEN TSE’I DZAY PA CHIK DÜ KYÖ
DRO WA KÜN GYI NYEN CHIK PU
JANG CHUP TSEN CHEN LA CHAK TSAL ^15-18

SÖ NAM TSA WA RAB TEN CHING
YE SHE TSE MO KA LA REK
DÖD GU’I CHAR BEP PAK SAM SHING
TRA SHI PAL Ö LA CHAK TSAL ^15-19

GANG GI SUNG RAP JI NYE PA
NAM CHÖ LO YI LEK DÜ TE
DRI MAY SUNG GI LEK GYUR WA
NGAK GI WANG CHUK LA CHAK TSAL ^15-20

TRA SHI KÜN GYI TRA SHI SHING
PAL KÜN GYI NI LEK PAR GYEN
NAM PAR DRAK LA NAM KÜN TU
CHOK TU DAY PAY TÖ PAR GYI ^15-21

KYÖ KYI KU YI Ö ZER GYI
MI TÜN CHOK LAY NAM GYAL TE
TÜN KYEN DRAK PAY DÜL JAY TSOK
DREN DZAY CHÖ JE LA CHAK TSAL ^15-22

GYA CHEN SÖ NAM CHU TSO’I Ü
KA GYÜ NOR BU’I PAL GYI TAM
TRIN LAY ZANG PO’I LAP TRENG YO
DRO WAY LA MA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-23

NAM KYEN GYAL WAY TU JE’I TÜ
TRA SHI NGO TSAR TRÜL PAY KU
NGÖN JÖN DRAK PAY GYAL TSEN CHEN
PAL GÖN ZANG PO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-24

NAM MANG GYAL WAY KYEN TSE’I TER
PUN TSOK LEK SHE GA TÖN GYI
DÜL JAR MIN DROL CHOK TSÖL WAY
YONG DZIN DAM PA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-25

GANG GI DRO KÜN GAB DE’I PAL
TRA SHI CHÖ CHAR BEP KAY SHING
NAM DRÖL NYU GU KYE DZAY PA
SEM PA CHEN PO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-26

KYAB DAK DE CHEN DOR JE’I KU
SHÖL MAY SI SHI KÜN GYI PAL
NAM KÜN TAK PA’I TRIN LAY CHEN
NGA WANG NAM GYAL LA CHAK TSAL ^15-27

NGA WANG SUNG RAP GYA TSO’I DZÖ
TRA SHI DRUP GYÜ TEN PA’I PAL
MAY JUNG TRIN LAY LHÜN DRUP PAY
CHOK TRÜL DAM PA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-28

TSOK NYI GYA TSO’I PAL GANG SHING
YE SHE TSÜL TRIM PUN SUM TSOK
LAR CHAY DRO WA’I KYAP CHIK PU
DRAK PA LEK DRUP LA CHAK TSAL ^15-29

DÜD DE’I PUNG CHEN RAB CHOM SHING
NYÖN MONG TOK TSOK MA LÜ SHI
KYEN RAP KYIL KOR YONG GANG WA
JAM PAL GYA TSO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-30

GYAL KÜN TU JE’I RANG ZUK LA
LUK NYI TEN PAY BA DEN CHOK
SI DIR NGÖN PAR DRENG DZAY PA
NGA WANG DRAK PA LA CHAK TSAL ^15-31

DO DANG NGAK KYI TUK TAM SHING
PAL DANG YÖN TEN DU MA’I TER
CHOK TÜN NGÖ KÜN TSÖL DZAY PA
TRIN LAY TEN DZIN LA CHAK TSAL ^15-32

PAY KAR CHANG WA DRO WAY GÖN
GANG DIR GYAL WA NYO PA SHIN
LÜ CHEN PEN DE’I PAL CHIK PU
DRAK PA RIN CHEN LA CHAK TSAL ^15-33

NGÖN JANG TSOK NYI CHU TER LAY
TSE CHEN YI SHIN RIN CHEN GYI
SI SHI’I ÜL WA KÜN SEL WA
NYEN DRAK PAL ZANG LA CHAK TSAL ^15-34

JI TA’I NAY LUK MA TRÜL ZIK
JI NYE SHE JA KÜN KYEN CHING
RANG SHEN DÖN NYI LHÜN DRUP PA
RIN CHEN LEK DRUP LA CHAK TSAL ^15-35

DRANG YAY GYAL WA GYA TSO YI
KYEN TSE NÜ SUM CHIK GYUR PA
TEN DI’I SHE NYEN DREN DA DREL
CHÖ YING LHÜN DRUP LA CHAK TSAL ^15-36

DÜ DIR NYING PO’I TEN PA DANG
CHOK CHU KYE WO’I TSA LAK CHE
SO KYE’I KAM SU DRAK BAR WA
CHÖ KYI PAL JOR LA CHAK TSAL ^15-37

LUNG TOK TEN PA’I GYAL TSEN CHOK
MI NUP SI PAY TSE MO RU
NAM PAR DRENG WAY JAY PO CHOK
DRAK PA GYA TSO LA CHAK TSAL ^15-38

DÜ SUM GYAL WA SAY CHAY KYI
KYEN TSE NÜ PAY DZAY TRIN KÜN
TUK KYI PAL BER RAB KHYIL WA
DRAK PA KÜN SAL LA CHAK TSAL ^15-39

KYEN TSE’I NYI MA BUM TRAK GI
SI SUM MONG PAY MÜN PA SEL
DÖN NYI LHÜN DRUP PAL YÖN CHEN
LO DRÖ DRI MAY LA CHAK TSAL ^15-40

SANG GYE KÜN GYI SANG WA SUM
CHIK TU DU PAY RANG ZUK NYID
DOR JE DZIN PAY KUR NANG WA
KÜN GA’I TSEN CHEN LA CHAK TSAL ^15-41

PANG TOK YÖN TAN GYAM TSO’I TER
SHEN LA PEN PAY TRIN LAY CHEN
LUNG TOK CHÖ TSÜL GYAM TSO’I TER
NGA WANG LEK DRUP LA CHAK TSAL ^15-42

DAK NI DÜ DI NAY ZUNG TE
JANG CHUP NYING POR CHI KYI BAR
SANG GYE KÜN NGÖ LA MA YI
TSOK NAM KÜN LA KYAB SU CHI ^15-43

CHI NANG SANG WA CHÖD PAY TRIN
LÜ DANG DE SHIN LONG CHÖ DANG
DÜ SUM GE WA CHI SAK PA
DE DAK KÜN LA KÜN NAY CHÖD ^15-44

TOK MA MAY DEN KOR WA NAY
MI SHE PA DANG MA GÜ DANG
BAK MAY NYÖN MONG WANG GYUR PAY
NYE PA GANG GYI TÖL SHING SHAK ^15-45

LA MA CHOK DANG GYAL WA DANG
GYAL WAY SAY DANG RANG GYAL DANG
NYEN TÖ SO SO KYE WO YI
GE WA KÜN LA JE YI RANG ^15-46

LO DRÖ MEN PAY TOK KAY CHIR
CHÖ SUNG SHE PAR MA GYUR PAY
DREN PA NAM LA DAK GI NI
CHÖ KYI KOR LO KOR WAR KÜL ^15-47

KOR WA JI SI NAY KYI BAR
NYA NGEN MIN DA TAK SHUK NAY
DAK CHAK NAM KYI DÖN DZAY PAR
SAM PA TAK PAY SÖL WA DEP ^15-48

GYAL WA SAY DANG CHAY NAM KYI
JI TAR YONG SU NGÖ PA SHIN
GE WA DI DANG GE WA SHEN
TAM CHAY JANG CHUP CHEN POR NGO ^15-49

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^15-50

## 16. This is the Supplication of the Kagyu called the Manika Crystal ^16-0

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^16-1

OM AH HUNG CHÖ YING NAM KA TA BU LA
ZUK KU CHEN POY KUR SHENG PA
TSEN PE RAB GYEN LONG CHÖ DZOK
TSEN RIK KÜN DRUK PA DOR JE CHANG
LA MA DE LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-2

DRAM ZE’I RIK LÜ KU TRUNG PA
KA BAP NAM PA DRUK GYÜD DZIN
MU TEK MANG DU TÜL DZAY NAY
DRUP GYÜD TEN PA RIN CHEN NI
DRÖN ME TA BUR GYE DZAY PAY
TE LO PA SHE DRAK PA YI
LA MA DE LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-3

DU NGAL KYE DU SAY JAY NAY
KA CHEN CHU NYI KA TREN CHAY
TE LO RAP TU NYE GYUR NAY
DAM CHÖ BUM PA GANG JÖL TOB
JA LÜ KA CHÖ NGÖ DRUP NYE
JNYA NA SID DHI SHE DRAK PAY
LA MA DE LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-4

PAL DEN GYE PA DOR JE DE
DRO LA NYING JE RAP ZIK NAY
TUK KAY HUNG LAY TRÖ PA YI
MAR TÖN CHÖ KYI LO DRÖ SHE
BÖD KAM CHÖ KYI GANG WAR DZAY
LA MA DE LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-5

JE TSÜN DE YI JE ZUNG WAY
LUNG SEM DHU TIR TSÜD GYUR NAY
GANG SHAR TAM CHAY CHÖ NYID TOK
SHEN RIK NGÖN PAR SHE PAY KYEN
DOR JE GYAL TSEN SHE DRAK PAY
PA RAY CHEN DE LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-6

SHAK TUB CHEN PÖ LUNG TEN PA
NYAL GYI CHOK SU KU TRUNG NAY
SO RIK MEN GYI LOB NYER DZAY
KA CHAK CHU WO NYI DRE KYI
GYÜD PA NAM NYI CHE PAR ZUNG
DZAM LING DRAK PA SHE DRAK PAY
LA MA DE LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-7

NYAM MAY DE YI JE ZUNG NAY
NUB CHIK KU YI TRÜL PA NI
JE WA TRAK GYAR GYE NÜ PAY
DRAK TSAL CHEN SOK DAM LA TAK
DOR JE GYAL PO SHE DRAK PAY
LA MA DE LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-8

CHOM DEN MI TRUK GÖN PO NYID
DRO MANG DE LA KÖD JAY CHIR
DAK CHAK NAM KYI DREN PA PO
TSEN YONG DRAK SHIN TU JÖD KA WA
JE DREN CHOK TRA SHI PAL SHE DRAK
PA LA MA DE LA SÖL WA DEP
PA KYE KYI TU JE NYUR DU ZUNG ^16-9

PAL DEN TAK LUNG KA GYÜ KYI
GYÜD PA RIN CHEN MA NOR WAR
ZIN PAY JE TSÜN DAM PA JE
DÖN GYÜ NAM LA SÖL WA DEP
JE YI JIN GYI LAP TU SÖL ^16-10

LA MA NAM KYI TU JE YI
JIN LAP GYÜD LA SHUK TU SÖL
YI DAM NAM LA SÖL WA DEP
CHOK TÜN NGÖ DRUP CHAR TAR POB ^16-11

CHÖ KYONG NAM LA SÖL WA DEP
MU TEK DÜD DE’I PUNG TSOK SHOM
NOR LHA NAM LA SÖL WA DEP
DÖD YÖN RAT NAY NGÖ DRUP TSÖL ^16-12

DI TSÖN PA YI GE WA YI
SANG GYE TEN PA RIN PO CHE
DE’I NYING DRUP GYÜ TEN PA DI
CHOK DÜ YÜL KAM TAM CHAY DU
KYAB CHING DAR GYE TAK PA DANG
TEN DZIN KU TSE TEN PA DANG
KYE GU CHÖ LA CHÖ PAR SHOK ^16-13

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^16-14

## 17. Knowing the Space of Objects of Knowledge ^17-0

SHE JAY KA LA KYEN RAP KYIL KOR GYE
DZAY PAY ÖD TONG DÜL JAY LING LA POK
GYAL WAY TEN PA KÜN NAY SAL DZAY PA
KA GYÜ RIN PO CHE LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-1

MI KOM GYE PANG DAL JOR PÜN TSOK TOB
DRUP PAY CHOK NYE GE JONG KÜN GYI TSO
TUB PAY GYAL TSAP GANG CHEN TSUK GI GYEN
NYAM MAY GAM PO PA LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-2

TSEN DEN LA MA RIN CHEN DRO WAY GÖN
DÜL JAY DÖN DU RI WO SHEN TIR JÖN
LUNG DANG TOK PAY CHÖ KYI SHEN DÖN DZAY
TÖN PAY GYAL TSAP KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-3

SA MO LUK LA TAM PA NAY ZUNG TE
TRUL PAY NAY SU SHEK PAR GYUR GYI BAR
RAP TU NGO TSAR NAM TAR PAK MAY NAM
DOR DÜ JÖ LA GÜ PAY NYEN PAR GYI ^17-4

ME TOK DA DZE PAY MAY LA MA NI
TUB PAY CHEN NGAR DA ÖD SHÖN NUR GYUR
GANG RI’I TRÖD DIR GE LONG TSO JAY NAY
GYAL WAY LUNG TEN KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-5

MÖN LAM DREL WAY NYAL GYI CHOK SU TRUNG
JIK TEN CHÖ TÜN TSÜN MO KAB TU SHE
SHEN LA PEN CHIR SO CHAY TAR CHIN DZAY
KYE WO JANG SEM KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-6

KOR WAY JA WA NYING PO MAY ZIK NAY
KYIM NAY JUNG TE TEN LA RAB TU JUNG
LAB SUM DRI MAY MIK DRAY SHIN DU SUNG
GE LONG TSO JE KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-7

SHE NYEN DAM PAY SHAB DÜL CHI WOR LANG
DÜL WA NGAK SOK SEN PA GYA CHEN DZAY
MI LAM SA TOB TING DZIN DU MA PEL
SÖ NAM RIN CHEN KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-8

MÖN SEM DAK PAY LA MAY TSEN TÖ TSE
BA PU YÖ SHING GA WA CHOK TU GYE
MI LAM TAY DANG TSEN MA ZANG DU JÖN
KYE BU LAY CHEN KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-9

JE TSÜN MI LAY DRUNG DU JÖN PAY TSE
KYÖ NI SEM CHEN KÜN GYI RIN PO CHE
SUNG NAY TUK SAY GYÜD DZIN YIN PAR GONG
Ü PA TÖN PA KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-10

CHOK TU ZAP PAY TAB LAM DAM PA NANG
YENG WA MAY PAR DRUP LA NEN TEN DZAY
LA MAY DRIN GYI TOK PA TUK LA TRUNG
KYE BU TSÖN DEN KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-11

SANG NGAK LA MAY CHOK GI LAM LA SHUK
TAK CHU YÖN TEN GYÜ SOK TAR CHIN DZAY
TSA LUNG TIG LE’I JE PA LAY SU RUNG
NAL JOR WANG CHUK KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-12

RIM NYI TÜN SHI’I NAL JOR GOM PAY TSE
MEN LA CHE DUN LONG KU’I SHING KAM DANG
TUB WANG LA SOK SANG GYE TONG DANG JAL
MI WANG LHÜN PO KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-13

JE TSÜN NYID KYI CHE DU NGAK PA JÖ
KU SUM NGÖN GYUR NGÖ DRUP NAM NYI NYE
KYÖ NI GE LONG DOR JE DZIN PA SHE
DZAM LING DRAK PAY SHAB LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-14

WANG DANG CHÖ NAM MA LÜ DZOK PAR NANG
DIK DROK PANG NAY WEN PA TEN PA DANG
SHEN DÖN DRUP PAY TSOK LA KYONG SHIK SUNG
KYE BU CHEN PO KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-15

ÖL KAR SHUK TSE CHÖ NAM MI LAM SHIN
DEN MAY TOK NAY TRÖ DRAL NGÖN DU GYUR
NGA YI KYE WAY TA MA DI YIN SUNG
SAB CHU WANG CHUK KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-16

DAK LA GAM PO’I NAY SU JÖN PAY TSE
LA MAY SUNG SHIN DRUP PAR TUK LA GONG
KAN DRO’I LUNG TEN SHEN DÖN GYI SHIK SUNG
CHÖ JE GAM PO PA LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-17

NÖ DANG DAN PAY LAY CHEN DU MA DÜ
LA MAY MEN NGAK DAM PA ZAB MO TEN
MIN CHING DROL WAY KYE BU PAK MAY DÜ
NYAM MAY LA MA CHOK LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-18

RE SHIK KU YI TRÜL PA NAM SHIR TEN
RAB TU NAY DANG SHEN LA CHÖ TÖN DANG
KA LA PUR DANG CHIK NI DAK JAR SHUK
NANG WA WANG GYUR KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-19

SEM JONG ZAB MO’I TING DZIN GOM PAY TSE
ZAY PAR ZIL NÖN PEL SHING GYUR WA DANG
KU LA DRIP MAY JIK TEN WANG POR TRÜL
TRÜL PAY KYEM CHOK KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-20

KAY DRUP DU MAY Ü SU SHUK PAY TSE
CHÖ KYI POB PAY SHEN NANG ZIL GYI NEN
NGA NI TEN PAY DAK PO YIN NO SUNG
MI YI SENG GE KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-21

MIK TRUL TA BU’I TING DZIN GOM PAY TSE
NAM KA KENG PAY JANG CHUB CHEN POR TRÜL
KU YI DZU TRUL SAM LAY DAY PA TEN
NANG TONG YER ME KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-22

DAY DEN NAM KYI KOR LO DOM PAR TONG
RAB NAY DZAY TSE JANG CHUP CHEN POR GYUR
YE SHE SEM PA TEN LA NGÖ SU TIM
DRUP PAY CHOK NYE KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-23

DAY DANG DEN PAY KYE WO GANG SHIK GI
KYE NI TONG TÖ DREN PA TSAM GYI KYANG
DU NGAL PANG NAY TAR PAY LAM NA ZIN
MEN PA’I GYAL PO KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-24

DÜD TSI TAB JOR CHAK GYA DZAY PAY TSE
LÜ NANG SEM SUM CHIK TU DRE PAY DAR
KU YI TRÜL PA KAY CHIK DRANG MAY TRÜL
DREN CHOK GYAL PO KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-25

SHEN DÖN SHEK PAR TUK LA TRUNG PAY TSE
CHU MO JA YI NYA DRUK TSO NGA’I NYIN
DAK NYID CHEN PO CHÖ KYI YING SU SHEK
NYA NGEN DA SHE KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-26

JA ÖD NAM MANG DUK DANG GYAL TSEN DANG
ME TOK CHAR BAP ROL MO’I DRA NAM JUNG
SA NAM YÖ SHING JA NAM CHA CHO DROK
NGO TSAR MAY JUNG KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-27

DAY DANG DEN PAY KYE WO PAK MAY DÜ
LHA MI’I CHÖD PA PÜL DU JUNG WAY CHÖD
TUK JAK RING SEL DAY PAY TEN DU SHAK
PEN GONG DZAY PA KYÖ LA CHAK TSAL LO ^17-28

RANG SHIN TRÖ DREL Ö SAL CHÖ KYI KU
DE CHEN NANG WA LHÜN DRUP LONG CHÖD DZOK
TRÜL PAY ZUK KYI DRO NAM MIN DROL DZAY
DREN CHOK LA MA GYÜD PAR CHAY NAM LA ^17-29

KÜN ZANG CHÖ TRIN NAM KA NYAM PAY CHÖD
LÜ NGAK YID KYI MI GE SO SOR SHAK
RANG SHEN KÜN GYI GE LA JE YI RANG
CHÖ KOR KOR SHING TAK TU SHUK PAR SÖL ^17-30

GÖN PO KYÖ NI JANG CHUB TSUL TEN TSE
TU JE’I CHEN GYI DRO LA ZIK NAY KYANG
DAK SOK DÜL JA YAL WAR MI DOR SHING
TEK CHOK DÜD TSI TSIM PAR DZAY DU SÖL ^17-31

SI PA KÜN TU LA MAY SHAB TEN CHING
NYE DANG KUR TI DRUP PAY CHÖ JAY LA
TÖN PAY DU SHE NAM YANG MIN DREL WAR
KYÖN GYI TOK PAY GÖ PAR MA GYUR CHIK ^17-32

LA MA’I DZAY PA JI TAR TEN NA YANG
NGO TSAR MÖ PAY RAB TU CHÖD JAY CHING
JA WA KÜN LA SHEN KYÖN MI TOK PAR
PEN PAY DU SHE TAK TU NYE GYUR CHIK ^17-33

DAK KYANG KYE SHIN NGÖN PAR JANG CHUP NAY
DÜL JAR GYUR PAY KA NYAM SEM CHEN LA
ZUNG DANG POB PAY CHÖ KYI GA TÖN GYE
TEK CHEN CHÖ KYI DRA YANG DROK PAR SHOK ^17-34

CHAK TSAL WA DANG CHÖ CHING SHAK PA DANG
JE SU YI RANG KUL SHING SÖL WA YI
GE WA CHUNG ZAY DAK GI CHI SAK PA
YÖD MAY LA SOK TA LA MI NAY PAR
KU SHI YE SHE NGA YI DAK NYID CHEN
LA MAY DZOK PAY JANG CHUP CHEN POR NGO ^17-35

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^17-36

DE SHIN SHEK PAY LUNG TEN CHING
NYIK MAY DÜ DIR DRO DÖN DZAY
CHAK GYA CHE LA NGA NYE PAY
DA Ö SHON NU LA CHAK TSAL ^17-37

PAL PAK MO DRU YI NAK TRÖD DU
KAL DEN DÜL JA MANG PO MIN DZAY CHING
SAM GYI MI KYAB CHO TRÜL NA TSOK TÖN
DRO GÖN RIN PO CHE LA SÖL WA DEP ^17-38

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^17-39

## 18. This is the Prayer Based on the Biography of the Protector of Beings Phagmo Drupa called the Pleasant Rain of the Amrita of Blessings ^18-0

NA MO GU RU ^18-1

NGÖN TSE DE SHIN SHEK PA KOR WA JIK
SHING DIR DZOK PAY SANG GYE SHAK YAY TOK
MA ONG ME TOK TRAM PAY JIK TEN DU
MI KYÖ DOR JE’I KUR GYUR CHÖ TÖN PAY
DRO GÖN RIN PO CHE LA SÖL WA DEP ^18-2

SANG GYE MA CHAK PAY MAY CHEN NGA RU
KYIM DAK PAL GYI KOR LO LA SOK PAY
GYUN TAY JANG SEM DRUP PAY LOP PÖN GYE
PEN DRUP GYAL LÖN TA MAL TSÜL DAK GI
TA YE DRO WA DREN DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-3

LAR YANG DA ÖD SHÖN NU’I GYAL TSAB TU
DO KAM DRI LUNG CHOK SU JIK PA NGE
TRÜL PAY KUR TEN KYE WAY DU NGAL DREL
TAM PA TAK TU DÜ SUM SA LER KYEN
TRÜL PAY KUM CHOK DAM PAR SÖL WA DEP ^18-4

TING DZIN RAP TRAM GYA TSOR JUK CHING DANG
GE WAY SHE NYEN TSÜL TRIM BAR WA DANG
Ö ZER GYAL TSEN DRUNG LAY RAP TU JUNG
DRO WAY GÖN PO DOR JE GYAL PO SHE
TSEN GYI CHÖ PEN CHANG DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-5

DRA DANG ZO SO DRANG NAY TSIK GYEN SOK
LAP LA MA TÖ NGANG GI KYEN GYUR CHING
DO KAM NYID NAY SHE NYEN CHU DRUK LAY
SHUNG DANG MEN NGAK SAM LAY DAY PA SEN
KAL DEN MIN DRÖL DZAY LA SÖL WA DEP ^18-6

NYER NYI SHE TSE Ü TSANG CHOK SU JÖN
TÖD LUNG GYA MAR CHÖ DRAR DEN PEP TE
DÜL NGÖN U TSAY PAR CHIN SHUNG MANG SEN
CHÖ TSÖ TSÖM LA MRA WAY DA DREL WA
GANG CHEN KAY PAY WANG POR SÖL WA DEP ^18-7

ZUR PU’I CHÖ DRAR JA DÜL CHEN PO SOK
TEN KAY YÖN TEN TSOK PAY KEN LOB PAY
NYEN POR DZOK TE DUR MRIK GYAL TSEN CHANG
TÖN PA NYI PAR GYUR PAY TSEN NYEN GYI
SI DIR DZE PAR JAY DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-8

JA YUL LA SOK TEK CHEN SHE NYEN LAY
MÖN JUK SEM KYE DOM PA DAK GIR SHE
TSE CHEN KUN GA NYING PO’I SHAL NGA NAY
NGAK GYÜ MEN NGAK GYA TSO TA BUR SEN
TSA LUNG TIG LE’I DRÖD TAK NGÖN DU GYUR
DOR JE DZIN PA CHEN POR SÖL WA DEP ^18-9

JANG CHUP SEM PA DA WA GYAL TSEN DANG
TSA MI LO TSA PAL CHEN GA LO SOK
KAY SHING DRUP PAY LOB PÖN PAK MAY LAY
GANG CHEN JONG DIR CHÖ TSUL YÖD DO CHOK
MA LÜ SEN SAM GOM PAY TUK GYÜ MIN
KAY DRUP CHEN POR GYUR DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-10

GYAL WAY LUNG TEN NYAM MAY GAM PO PAY
SHAB PAY NYE PA SUM GYI CHI WÖ CHÖD
CHÖ NYID DEN PA MA NOR NGÖN SUM ZIK
NGE DÖN ZAB GYE DÜT SI’I KU NYAM TAR
LA MAR PANG TOK NYAM PAR SHAL GYI SHE
CHÖ KYI GYAL PO CHEN POR SÖL WA DEP ^18-11

DAK PAY NANG WA DAM PA SANG GYE DANG
MA CHIK JAL TE DU NGAL SHI JAY KYI
DAM NGAK MA LÜ BUM PA GANG JOR NANG
SHIB CHU TSA GU SA PO TAK GI LO
KÜN TU ZANG PO’I NAK TRÖD PAK MO DRUP
CHÖ KYI SENG TRIR SHUK DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-12

TSANG PAY SUNG GI DÜT SI LA SAY PAY
KAL ZANG DÜL JA DRANG LAY DAY PA DAK
MAY JUNG DAM PAY CHÖ KYI TRIM LA NAY
PAK PA NYE WAR KOR SHIN LAP NAY SUNG
DAK SHEN JE WAY JANG SEM SHI SHING DÜL
SEM PA CHEN PO CHOK DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-13

DE CHEN DOR JE GAR GYI NAM PAR GYE
NYAM NYID TING DZIN GYAL PO NGÖN JANG CHUP
KYAP CHING TAK PAY TRIN LAY ZAY MI SHE
RAP JAM CHÖ DZÖ GYA TSOR WANG YANG GYUR
NAY LUK DE TONG CHAK GYA CHEN PO YI
KAL ZANG MIN DROL DZAY DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-14

BU CHEN NYER NGA DUK TOK NGAB GYA DANG
TSOK PA GYE GYA LA SOK DRANG LAY DAY
ZAB CHÖ TÖ TSAY MI DRÖL JUNG MI SI
CHÖ KYI KOR LO KÜN GYI NYING PO’I CHÜD
DÖN ZANG TSIK DRU ZANG DEN KA LA NYAM
TEN CHÖ TEN PAY TSAY SU TSAM DER DÜD ^18-15

SANG WA SUM GYI DZU TRUL SAM LAY DAY
TEN DREL NA RE TSEN DRO’I DÖN CHEN DZAY
KAL DEN SO SO’I WANG PO NGÖN LA MA
YONG SU DRUP PA NUP CHIK KU LÜ KYI
KÖ PA CHU NYI TEN DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-16

SHING DI’I DÜL JA PAK MAY SI TSO LAY
DRÖL WAR DZAY DE DRUK CHU TSA CHIK PA
TAK LO MIN DA’I NYER NGAR ZUK KYI KU’I
KÖD PA DÜ NAY DÜL DRAL SHING KAM SU
MI KYÖ GYAL WAY CHEN NGAR JANG CHUP SEM
LO DRÖ DRI MAY GYAL POR TEN DER DÜD ^18-17

KU DUNG JANG TSE RIK NGA’I DE SHEK JÖN
PA WO PA MO’I TSOK KYI CHÖD CHING TÖD
TUK JAK CHEN SUM RING SEL SAM LAY DAY
SA YÖ ME TOK CHAR BAB JA TSÖN TRIK
CHÖ KU’I CHÖ DONG SÖ NAM SHING DU TRÜN
NGO TSAR SAM LAY DAY DER SÖL WA DEP ^18-18

DE TAR TONG TÖ DREN PAY DÖN DEN PAY
CHÖ KYI GYAL PO DRO GÖN CHEN PO YI
NAM TAR NYING NAY DREN SHING SÖL DEP NA
CHÖ YING NYAM PA NYID LAY TSE CHEN GYI ^18-19

TU JE CHEN PO’I TRIN TSOK CHER TRIK TE
GÖN PO KYE KYI SUNG LA JE LOB PAY
DAK SOK DÜL JA’I LO GYÜ SHING SA LA
JIN LAB DÜD TSI DRANG CHAR NYUR PAB NAY
DE CHEN KU SHI’I DRAY ZANG MIN DZAY SÖL ^18-20

CHÖ KYI GYAL PO KYE KYI CHÖ DRA DIR
MI SUN LOK PAR TA WAY DÜD PUNG TÜL
CHI NANG SANG WAY BAR CHAY MA LÜ SÖL
NAY LUK DE TONG DÖN LA NYAM SHAK PAY
NGE DÖN DÜL JA’I CHU TER GYE PAR DZÖ ^18-21

GYAL KAM KUN TU DU KYI GYUD PA SHI
KÜN KYANG GAY CHU’I LAM LA CHIK TU TSÖN
TAM CHAY CHAM CHIK DOR JE GYAL PO YI
GO PANG DAM PA NYUR DU TOB GYUR CHIK ^18-22

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^18-23

## 19. This is the Praise of the Life Story of Glorious Taklung Tangpa called the Great Drum of Sincere Faith ^19-0

JE KU SHI’I WANG CHUK DOR JE CHANG
PAL TAK LUNG TANG PAY SHAB LA DÜD
CHIR SHE JA’I PÜN TSOK JI NYE PA
JE KYE KYI TU JE NYID LAY JUNG ^19-1

DÖN DE CHIR JE JUK DÜL JA NAM
CHÖ CHIK CHOK GI GYAL PO SÖL DEP YIN
SÖL DEP LA NGE SHE KYE JE DE
PA LA MA’I NAM TAR TONG WAY YIN ^19-2

NAM TAR GYI ZAB SANG LAB PO CHE
KONG PAK CHEN GA MA TOK PA
DAK TSÜR TONG GI CHÖ YÜL GA LA YIN
DÖN YIN LUK CHOK TSAM SHAY TSA NA ^19-3

NAY OG MIN CHÖ YING PO DRANG DER
TSEN CHÖ KU DRUK PA DOR JE CHANG
RI PO TA LA YI TSE MO NA
GÖN JIK TEN WANG CHUK CHEN RE ZIK ^19-4

PAL NUP CHOK OR GYEN SHING KAM NA
CHOK DRUP PAY TSO WO WANG PO’I LO
SHAR DÜL DANG DREL WAY SHING KAM NA
JE DZOK PAY SANG GYE MI TRUK GÖN ^19-5

CHIR CHOK CHU’I SHING KAM TA YE NA
GÖN GYAL WA SAY CHAY PAK PAY TSOK
DE TAM CHAY CHÖ JE LA MA YI
KU DZU TRUL KÖD PAY DÖ GAR YIN ^19-6

NGE TÜN MONG GI DÜL JAY NANG WA LA
GÖN SHAK YAY GYAL PO’I DÜL WAY SHING
PAL MI JE DZAM LING JANG CHOK DIR
KU TRÜL PAY NANG NYEN JÖN PA’I TSÜL ^19-7

YID MÖ PAY TEN DU DOR DU TSAM
NGAK DRO KUR KYÖN GYI MA LAY PA
JE RIN CHEN NYID KYI SHAL NGA NAY
SUNG SENG GE’I DRA CHEN DI TAR DRAK ^19-8

NGA’I YÜL NI YANG SHÖ TANG KYA YIN
RÜ NI CHE SUM GA GZI’I RIK
YAB NI BEN LÖN BAR PO DANG
YÜM NI GE SUM TRO MO YIN ^19-9

NGA RANG DÜ SUM SANG GYE KYI
KU SUNG TUK DANG YER MI CHAY
CHE DÜ SUM GYAL WAR SHAL GYI SHE
DRI LOK SOK JANG PAY TSÜL DZAY CHING
PAL TANG KYA’I GÖN DU RAB TU JUNG ^19-10

KEN PO GA TÖN LHA KANG PA
LOB PÖN TSAR TÖN GO MANG PA
GUK GE DRAK GÖN LA SOK PAR
CHÖ TÖ PAY DRO DOK MANG DU CHAY ^19-11

LA TÖ KYI NAL JOR DRUP CHEN LAY
LAM SHI JAY DRÖN MA NAM SUM SHÜ
DÜ DE TSE DRO GÖN CHÖ JE YI
TSEN TÖ PA TSAM GYI MÖ GÜ BAR ^19-12

NGÖN Ü SU JÖN DRAP MANG PO DZAY
DE ÖN KYANG SHOL NAY MA TÖN PAY
DÜ DE TSE RIN CHEN TRÜL PA NI
JE GOL PÜ KYEN GYI TSAM JAR NAY
PE GÖD PO’I NAM PANG CHÖ PA SHIN
DÖN TOK PA MAY PA Ü SU JÖN ^19-13

PAL KÜN ZANG PAG DRU’I NAK TRÖD NAY
NAY YANG GÖN CHÖ KYI PO DRANG TONG
DE TONG WAY MÖ LA BA PU YÖ
MI KO WAY SAM PA TAR CHIN NYAM
SHIN TU GYOK PAR CHEN NGAR CHIN ^19-14

DE SHEK TRI LA SHUK PA TONG
KÖN PAY RIN CHEN SER CHE PÜL
GÜ PAY CHAK TSAL KU KAM SHÜ
BU SHIN TU DE WAR YÖD DAM SUNG ^19-15

KYÖ BAR CHAY MAY PAR CHIN PA DI
MI KO WÖ TU JE YIN NO SUNG
NGA DOR JE DEN DU YÖ GYUR KYANG
KYÖ NGA CHIR DRANG GÖN SHIK YIN SUNG ^19-16

DE’I NUP NYE NAY NAM PA LA
DA CHI SAR PA JÖN DÜ SU
SAR PAY LÜ LAY SAR PA CHAY
KO WOY TUK KAR TIM MO SUNG ^19-17

CHÖ LA MAY NAL JOR CHIK NANG WAY
CHOK TÜN MONG GI NGÖ DRUP MA LÜ PA
JUNG TRUNG DE MÖ GÜ CHIK PUR SHE ^19-18

SER SHO GANG GI MANDAL PÜL NAY KYANG
NGA SHU YAY NAL JOR DRA SHIK JAY
DÖN NYAM LEN MÖ GÜ CHIK PÜ CHOK
CHÖ NAM DRANG MANG PO MI GÖ SHÜ ^19-19

JE RIN CHEN GYE PAY SHAL NGA NAY
DE MÖ GÜ KYI KAM SO NÜ PA YIN
LAR KYE WA DI NYID KO NA MIN
DÜ TOK MAY NAY RANG RE DREL WA ZAP
BU KYÖ KYI YÖN TEN KYE JE DE
KO WO CHIK PU YIN NO SUNG ^19-20

NGAR JAL NYID NAY TUK YID CHIK TU DRE
DA DUNG KYANG MAN DAL YANG YANG PÜL
TSE CHIK TU SÖL WA TOB CHIK DANG
YÖN TEN GYI NAM DRANG MANG DU CHAR ^19-21

MI KO WOY TEN PAY DAK PO DE
BU KYÖ KYI NGE PAR JA GÖ PAY
CHÖ GANG NANG SHÜ LA GANG ZIN ZUNG
DU CHI NAY GÖ PAY KAB SHIK YONG
SHE LUNG TEN WANG DANG JIN LAP DANG
LUNG MEN NGAK ZAB TRI SHAL DAM CHAY
CHÖ TAM CHAY BUM PA GANG JÖ NANG ^19-22

LAM TAR TUK MÖ GÜ NAY DU TEB
TSE DRUP PA NYAM PAY SHAL DAM CHAY
JE SANG GYE NGÖ SU TONG WA LA
TA MAL DU DZIN PA GA LA SHIK ^19-23

SAB CHU TSAM DU’ANG TRÜL MA NYONG
DÖN DE CHIR TONG CHAK TSAL WA LAY
GYAB TEN PAY LÜ KYI CHÖD LAM PANG
DU RE SHIK BA CHAK NYING KAR TAY ^19-24

CHÖ NYEN GYÜ YID SHIN NOR BU DI
KONG KAL MAY NAM LA JAK MA JUK
NGÖN JE TSÜN RIN CHEN SHÜK DU SU
MI KO WO TSAM LAMA TOK PA ^19-25

KONG SHEN MANAM LA NANG WA MAY
DE CHÖ LASER NA MA YIN TE
GYÜ NÖD DANG DEN LA GONG PA YIN
NYEN GYÜ YI GER MI DRI DANG ^19-26

DA GYÜ TSIK TU MI TÖN DANG
NYAM NYONG TROM LU MI LEN CHING
GE JONG NYÖ TAB MI JE PAR
GÖN SANG GYE LA MAT SUNG SHIN DU ^19-27

DÖN TRÜL MAY NYAM SU LANG JAY NAY
NÖ DÜL JAY WANG POY JE DRAK TAR
TUK CHÖ KU’I YE SHE NGÖN DU JAY
CHE CHE DU DAM NAY TRI TENG SHAK
GÖ SAR PA GYÖN LA DIR DÖ CHIK
KYÖ TEN PAY DAK PO YIN NO SUNG ^19-28

DÜ LAY MAR CHE TSÜN MANG PO YI
TSUK GYEN KYI DREN PA GÖ PAY CHIR
KEN LOB SOK TÜN KYEN TSANG WA LAY
TEN GE LONG GI NGÖ PO DRUP SHIK SUNG ^19-29

YAB CHÖ JE’I KA YI KUL WA SHIN
KEN CHEN SHA MA RA PA DANG
LOB PÖN MAR PA CHOM TANG DRUNG
TSANG CHÖ KYI TÜL SHIK DZOK PAR LANG ^19-30

YANG RIN CHEN SHAL NAY DI KAY DU
BU KYÖ LA DÜ PA DI NYE DANG
CHOK PANG TOK NYAM PA DI DAK DANG
SHING DI NA TSANG GYA’I LUNG TEN DZAY ^19-31

YENG DREL GYI RI TRÖ GÖN PA DER
SHEN MAY KYI DRO WAY DÖN LAY DU
SÖL DEP KYI DAM NGAK DRUP PAR JAY
MA DRO DRUK GI DREN LEN DRUP PAY JAL ^19-32

GÖD TSANG GI DRAK LA DAK JAR JAY
CHI JA JAY KYI NAM YENG GYÜN DRÜL DANG
NANG ZUNG DZIN GYI TAK PA BAY KYI CHAY
ÖD SAL GYI SEM LA NYAM JE MAY ^19-33

PAL CHEN PO TE LO’I CHIN CHAY DANG
NGA BAN CHUNG TRA PAL YEN CHAY DU
DE SHEK KYI GYÜD PA MA TOK PA
KONG SO KYE’I BAR CHAY MAY DO SUNG ^19-34

NGÖN DAY PAY SANG GYE MAR ME DZAY
CHI MA ONG GYAL WA JAM PA GÖN
KAB DA TA’I DREN PA SHAK YAY TOK
DÜ SUM GYI GYAL WAR SHAL GYI SHE ^19-35

DE MÖ KYANG DÜL JA DÜL TAB SU
TEN RAB TU JUNG WA TSÜN CHAY DU
SHA CHANG LA CHAK GI REK PA MAY
ZIM KANG DU BÜ MAY ONG SA MAY ^19-36

KONG CHE NYEN GYI GE SHE TSÜN CHAY DU
MA NÜ KYI LONG TRANG YAN CHAY LA
CHÖD JIN KAY SA NAK TSANG NAY
JA TSAM GYI ZA TUNG NYAM PAR NANG ^19-37

CHI JUNG SHI ZA KAR DÜ TSIK DANG
NANG TSA YIK LUNG SEM KOR LO DANG
SANG RIK PO TÖL JUNG TUK TRAY NAM
CHÖR TEN DREL DAK PAY KOR LOR SHAR ^19-38

JI SI SHI’I PAL GYUR RIN PO CHE
YAB DRO WAY GÖN DANG NYI SU MAY
KONG LAY CHEN DAK PAY DÜL JA NAM
YID DAK NANG GI TONG TSÜL NA TSOK TE
KONG LA LAY GYAL WA RIK NGAR TONG
LA LAY TU JE CHEN POR TONG ^19-39

LA LAY TEN PAY DAK POR TONG
LA LAY DEM CHOK KOR LOR TONG
MANG PO’I DE SHEK GONG MAR TONG
LA LAY KU CHIK MANG POR TONG ^19-40

RIN CHEN NAM NYI SHAL DZOM NAY
CHÖ KYI DRO LENG DZAY PAR TONG
DE TAM CHAY RIN CHEN NAM TRÜL YIN
RIN CHEN KYANG SEM KYI NAM TRUL YIN ^19-41

DÖN DE CHIR LA MA RANG GI SEM
CHIR RIN CHEN JE DANG JAL CHIN CHAY
CHI SUNG CHI GYE DRUP PAR JAY ^19-42

KU TONG WAY DÜ SU DZUM GYI SÜ
SUNG TÖ PAY DÜ SU MÖ GÜ JAY
DZAY CHÖ NAM TAR TAM CHAY LA
TRÜL PAY KU RU NGE SHE KYE ^19-43

CHIR KOR DAY KYI DÜL JA DRANG YAY KYANG
NGE TÜN MONG GI NANG WAR DÜ PAY TSOK
TÖN KOR LA YE WA MAY PA YI
SAY PANG TOK NYAM PA MANG DU JÖN ^19-44

GYA NAG TU KO WO CHEN PO YI
KOR DÜ PA GYA TRAK DÜN DU KYANG
GYA YÜL DU BAY JE GYA TRAK NGA
JANG A GÖ SHIK PÖ BUM TRAK DANG ^19-45

JE DZA GÖN CHEN PÖ TONG TSA SUM
YUL GO SHÖ RAY PAY TONG TRAK NYI
PU BOR TSAM SAY GYA TRAK SUM
ME NYAK GI GHA RANG GYA TRAK NGA ^19-46

LA TÖ NA DRA NAK SENG GE DANG
DO KAM KYI RAY CHEN BOM GÖN NYI
RE RE SHIN GYA TRAK SUM SUM KYANG
ME NYAK TU SUM BU SER TSANG PAR
KOR TOK DEN GYI LOB MA DRANG MAY DÜ ^19-47

MU TO DANG GA TÖN LUNG KA SUM
JE NYID DANG DREL MAY SHAL GYI SHE
SHANG O SHA DANG ÖN GYI TSI LUNG PAS
KOR DÜ PA MANG PO MIN PAR DZAY ^19-48

JANG SA RAM GYI NYE MO DZE GOM GYI
DZA TRÜL GYI CHAK KYE NYIN SHIN DZAY
KA CHE NA MA YO GOM NAK GI
PAL DOR JE DEN GYI KEN PO DZAY ^19-49

RU TOK NO KONG PO GOM NAK GI
TAK LUNG DU GUR GUM POR GYE JAY
JAR NYAL NA SANG GYE RONG TÖN SHE
CHÖ DÖN GYÜD KYI TUK YID CHIK TU DRE ^19-50

SENG GA LAR LHUK GE GON CHEN DANG
NAY DZA LEN DHA RAR MA NI PA
OR GYEN NA DRAK PA GYAL TSEN DANG
A SU RAR LA MA GAR PA SOK
TUK NYE WAY SAY CHEN CHOB GYE PO
RE RE KYANG DÜ PA DRANG LAY DAY ^19-51

DE DOR DÜ TSAM DU JÖ PA YIN
CHIR TONG TÖ DREN REK TAM CHAY DANG
SHING KOR DAY KYI DÜL JA MA LÜ PA
GYÜD JANG CHUP CHEN POR MIN PAR DZAY ^19-52

JE SHING KAM SHEN LA GONG PAY TSE
MÖ DEN GYI LO MA YÖN DAK LA
GÖN CHÖ KYI GYAL PÖ DI KAY SUNG
BU KYÖ NAM SEM LA DI TAR SHOK ^19-53

KYÖ MÖ GÜ KYI SÖL WA DEP PA DANG
ZANG ZING GI SHAB TOK JAY PA NAM
MI DAK DANG DREL WAY TSENG PA YIN
NGE RIN CHEN JE LA SÖL WA TAB PAY
KOR DAY KYI PÜN TSOK JUNG WA YIN
KYE NAM KYANG SÖL WA JUNG WA TOB ^19-54

MI NGA LA RE TÖ CHAY PA NAM
JE RAT NA DANG YER MAY KO WÖ DREN
SHE LUNG TEN ÜK JUNG ZENG TÖD NAY
NYAM TOK PAY NGÖ DRUP KÜN GYI TOB ^19-55

JE DOR JE’I KU NYE TAK PAY KU
DRO ONG DANG SHEK SHUK MI NGA YANG
SHAR DAK PAY SHING CHOK NGÖN GA DER
GÖN MI KYÖ GYAL POR SHAL SHE NAY
KOR TER BUM DRANG MAY DÜ PA LA
CHÖ TEK CHEN KOR LO GYUN CHAY MAY ^19-56

GÖN TU JE’I ROL PA TSAY MAY KYI
DÜ CHI MAY MU TAR TUK GI BAR
JE NYID KYI TSÖD MAY TRÜL PAY KU
WÖN RIM PAR JÖN PAR JIN LAP SÖL ^19-57

JE CHER BAK CHER TSENG RIN PO CHE
CHUNG BAK CHUNG TSER RIN PO CHE
DREL TSAY DÖN DEN RIN PO CHE
KOR DAY KYI PAL GÖN RIN PO CHE ^19-58

DAK SÖL DEP DANG KA NYAM GYI MA GEN LA
YAB TSAY MAY TU JE’I PUNG KYE NAY
DÜ DREL MAY TSE WAY JE ZUNG TE
LAM TEK CHOK NGAK KYI DRÖD PA YIN
GÖN KYÖ DANG TUK KYID CHIK DRE NAY
MA DRO DRUK GI DREN PA NYID GYUR CHIK ^19-59

CHÖ YID SHIN GYI NOR BU JANG CHUP SEM
GANG SÖL WA TAB LA GÖ DÖ JUNG
TSAL TU JE’I TRA SHI BUM TRAK GI
SI SHI WAY KYÖN KÜN GE GYUR CHIK ^19-60

CHÖ DRUP GYÜD KYI TEN PA RIN CHEN DI
SHING RAB JAM GYA TSOR YONG KYAB NAY
MA RIK PAY LAY NYÖN KÜN SAL TE
YE SHE KYI NANG WA TAK NAY SHOK ^19-61

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^19-62

## 20. Condensed Birth Stories of Thangpa ^20-0

OG MIN SHING NA GYAL WA DOR JE CHANG
DRUP PAY NAY NA IN DRA BHA NI TE
GANG CHEN TRÖ DIR NYAM MAY TRA SHI PAL
LONG DRAK TANG PA CHEN PO KYÖ CHAK TSAL ^20-1

DÜL DREL SHING DU MI TRUK GÖN PO DANG
PO TA LA NA JAM GÖN CHEN RAY ZIK
GANG CHEN TRÖ DIR NYAM MAY TRA SHI PAL
YONG DRAK TANG PA CHEN PO KYÖ CHAK TSAL ^20-2

RA SA TRÜL NANG TRÜL PAY GYAL PO DANG
U RU TÖD DU PAK CHOK JA YUL WA
TAK LUNG TANG DIR NYAM MAY TRA SHI PAL
YONG DRAK TANG PA CHEN PO KYÖ CHAK TSAL ^20-3

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^20-4

## 21. Praise of the Previous Lives of TaklungThangpa Rinpoche ^21-0

GANG TUK CHÖ YING CHIK TU DAL GYUR KYANG
YE SHE CHEN PO’I GYU TRÜL NA TSOK KYI
NAM KAY TA LAY DRO WA MIN DZAY PA
PAL DEN TAK LUNG TANG PAR SÖL WA DEP ^21-1

SHAR CHOK DÜL DANG DREL WAY SHING KAM SU
JANG CHUP SEM PAY GEN DÜN DRANG MAY LA
TEK CHEN CHÖ KOR GYUN MI CHAY KOR WAY
GYAL WA MI TRUK GÖN PO KYÖ LA TÖ ^21-2

GYAL SAY KÜN GYI TU JE CHIK DÜ TE
MI JAY SHING DI’I DREN PA NGÖN DRUP NAY
DZY TOB TRIN LAY SAM LAY DAY PA YI
GÖN PO CHEN RAY ZIK WANG KYÖ LA TÖ ^21-3

OR GYEN CHÖ KYI GEN DZO DAM PA NAY
TÖN PAY KU SUNG TUK KYI SANG DZIN CHING
KA DU RIK NGAK CHANG WAY KOR LO’I GYUR
YE SHE LÜ CHEN IN DRA BHU TIR DÜ ^21-4

TUB PAY SHAB DRING NYEN TÖ GYA TSO YI
NANG NAY TÖ PA DZIN PAY CHOK GYUR PA
TA YAY DO DE’I DE NÖ DY WA PO
MANG TÖ PAK PA KÜN GA WO LA DÜ ^21-5

NYAM MAY TEN PAY DAP PO GYAL TSAB CHOK
NAY TEN CHEN PO DAK GI RIM DRO PA
NAM SUM DÜD SI DRIP SUM TSAY DUNG SEL
GE NYEN DAM PA DHAR MA TA LAR DÜ ^21-6

BÖ YUL GANG CHEN JONG DIR GE CHU YI
KA TRUM SER GYI NYA SHING TA BU DANG
DAM PAY CHÖ KYI DRÖL KA TÖ DZAY PA
SONG TSEN GAM POR TEN PA KYÖ CHAK TSAL ^21-7

ZAB MO TA WA GYA CHEN CHÖ PA DANG
NYAM LEN JIN LAB GYÜ PAY MEN NGAK GI
DRÖN ME’I BÖD YÜL LHAM MER SEL DZAY PA
DROM TÖN GYAL WAY JUNG NAY DE LA DÜ ^21-8

NYAM SUM LA MAY SHAB TOK LA TEN NAY
DZAY TRIN TEN DREL NGANG GI DRIK GYUR CHING
KYEN TOK TU JE SAM LAY DAY PA YI
GE WAY SHE NYEN JA YÜL WA LA DÜ ^21-9

CHI RU SO SOR TAR PAY LAP JA DANG
NANG DU DOR JE TEK PAY KYE DZOK KYI
NYING PO’I DÖN GYI TEN PO’I SOK SHING GYUR
GÖN PO MAN GA LA SHIR GÜ PAY DÜ ^21-10

DENG NAY LA MAY JANG CHUB MA TOB BAR
TSUNG DREL JE TSÜN TAK LUNG TANG PA YI
NAM PAR TAR LA JE SU LOB GYUR TE
GANG GI DOR JE’I SANG SUM DRUP PAR SHOK ^21-11

[Ed: no transliteration given in source] ^21-12

## 22. A Supplication Prayer Recounting the Liberating Life of Sangye WönDrakpa Pal Özer Zangpo, the Emanation of the Supreme Venerable Master ^22-0

NAMO RATNA GURU HE BADZRA YA ^22-1

DREN MAY GÖN KYÖ NAM KAY YING NAY JÖN
RIK NI GYUR MAY DOR JE CHANG GI RIK
KAN DRO’I LUNG TEN JO SHAK NYI LA KÜL
TA LAY TU JE CJEN LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-2

LHAM SU SHUK KYANG SI PAY CHAK DANG MAY
SAM SHIN KU TRUNG DRO WAY DÖN LA GONG
GYE DZAY NAL JOR TÜN DRUK YEL MAY DZAY
RIM NYI JANG PA CHEN LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-3

YUM LA MA NÖ DE WAY NAM PAR TSAY
TE WA CHAY PAY DRA LAY PI WAM GYUR
CHÖ KYI TSIK CHAY NYU SHU TSA CHIK SUNG
ZAY TSANG SAY DRA KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-4

JA ÖD NANG DU KA LA PAK NAY SHUK
AN JAY NGAK DANG ALI KALI SUNG
SHIN TU SONG SHING CHE PA YUM LA SUNG
BAY TSUL DZAY PA KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-5

DAK PAY SA CHOK Ü SU DEN DRANG TSE
PA WO’I LUNG TEN GYE DZAY KYUNG SHOK ZIK
LO SUM LÖN NAY DOR JE’I LU DZÖ DÖL
KYE PAR PAK PA KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-6

GYU TRÜL SAY DRA ROL TSAY DU MA DZAY
YI GE’I JUK GO DRA RIK DU MA SUNG
GYAL WAY KU SUK ZO NA DU MA TEN
JANG PA TAR CHIN KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-7

SA TREK DO LA MAR SHIN LU ZUK ZÖ
LEN JAY CHU LA KU DUNG SA TEN TAB
TING PAK KA WAY SA LA TOK TAR DZÜL
DREN TA DREL WA KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-8

TUR BAP CHU NU GYEN DU ME SHIN DOK
YANG SA’I DRAK LA LAM TAR GYUR MAY SHEK
NYAL TAK BU LA LHAK PAR TUK TSE DZAY
GYAL SAY CHOK GYUR KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-9

TRÖ SHING DZUP GANG TSEN MO RANG SAR BAR
JIN LAP KYIL KOR NAM KA’I YING SU TEK
MA DA’I DAM DZAY ZAK MAY DÜD TSIR GYUR
MIK TRÜL JI SHIN DZAY LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-10

TSEN MO CHIK LA OR GYEN NAY CHEN KOR
DRUP TOB YONG DRAK GYAY LA WANG DZOK SHÜ
NAM GYAL LÖN PA SA LU’I NYE MA NANG
NGÖ DRUP NGO TSAR CHEN LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-11

NGA DRO GYAL LÖN TSOM BU KYONG TSUL TEN
GONG MO RAP JUNG CHÖ KOR KOR WAR DZAY
SHAL CHAB DÜD TSI BU RAM RO DRA NANG
DRO DÖN DU MA DZAY LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-12

DRAK GI LOK LAY TAM DRIN KUM CHOK TÖN
LU SHING DRA MAY NÖD JIN SHUK KYI DÜL
NYEN KAR NAK PO’I SOK NYING NGÖ SU PÜL
TU TOB NGA WA KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-13

DRUP TOB NAY CHEN GA YI TER GO CHI
ME TAB Ü SU GYE DZAY LHA DU TEN
TEN DREL TAB KYI YÜL GYI TSE WA DOK
JIN LAP TAR CHIN KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-14

YE SHE KAN DRO’I DUNG LU NGÖN DU KÜL
DRI CHEN DA DÖN GYÜ DANG TÜN PAR TRÖL
KAN DRO GYA TSÖ TSÜK GI GYEN DU KÜR
DA DÖN NGA WA KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-15

TÖN PAY GYAL TSAB DAM PAR RAB JÖN CHING
TSEN CHOK DÖN DEN DRAK PAY PAL JOR GYA
YAB SAY YER MAY DZU TRÜL ÖD ZER GYE
TSEN PAY ZANG PO KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-16

NYI GYA NGAB CHU’I NYE TUNG KYÖN DANG DREL
DAK SHEN JE NÜ JANG SEM LO JANG JONG
RIK NYI ZAB ,O’I KYE DZOK NGÖN DU GYUR
DOM SUM TAR CHIN KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-17

SANG GYE NAY NI NYEN RANG YEN CHAY KYI
TONG NANG DU MA LAY CHEN NAM LA TAY
TSÖ PA DREL WAY NGE SHE TING NAY KYE
TÖ TSAY DÖN DEN KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-18

NA RO’I TAB LAM TSA LUNG NAY DU NÜN
YO JAY LUNG NI DHU TI’I NANG DU TSÜD
GANG SHAR TAM CHAY MÖ NYID CHEN POR ZIK
TONG NYID TUK DEN KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-19

GYAL WA GYAM TSO KÜN GYI KYIL KOR ZIK
GYA BÖD KAM DRUP KÜN GYI GONG PA KYEN
NAY SUM KAN DRO KÜN KYI LUNG TEN TOB
DRO DÖN LHÜN DRUP KYE LA SÖL WA DEP ^22-20

TRÜL TSAB CHEN LA LHAK PAR TÜL SHUK TEN
DO KAR YU WA BAL KÜD TA BUR DRIL
